📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期乙型肝炎病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 年龄较大、糖尿病的存在以及较低的血清白蛋白水平与对乙型肝炎疫苗接种的不良或无反应显著相关(p<0.05)。 [PMID:42811835]。
  • 总共纳入 28 448 例患者,其中 19 001 例(66.8%)依从性较高,9447 例(33.2%)依从性较低。 [10.1002/jmv.71167]。
  • NHANES中较高的ln(WHR)与肌少症独立相关(调整后OR = 2.50;95% CI:2.01–3.10),并在KNHANES中一致验证(OR = 1.85;95% CI:1.49–2.29)。 [10.1111/ggi.70858]。
  • 与HBV-DNA阴性组相比,HBV-DNA阳性与肝损伤加重显著相关,表现为ALT、AST升高,淋巴细胞计数增加以及纤维化评分(APRI和FIB-4)升高。无论HBV-DNA状态如何或肝病阶段(慢性乙型肝炎或肝硬化),EBV-DNA阳性均与血浆白蛋白(Alb)水平和白蛋白-球蛋白(A/G)比值显著降低相关,与EBV-DNA阴性患者相比。A/G比值的降低归因于白蛋白水平的下降,而非球蛋白(Glb)水平的升高。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Older age, presence of diabetes mellitus, and lower serum albumin were significantly associated with poor or non-response to hepatitis B vaccination (p<0.05). [PMID:42811835].
  • A total of 28 448 patients were included, of whom 19 001 (66.8%) showed high adherence and 9447 (33.2%) showed low adherence. [10.1002/jmv.71167].
  • Higher ln(WHR) was independently associated with sarcopenia in NHANES (adjusted OR = 2.50; 95% CI: 2.01–3.10) and consistently validated in KNHANES (OR = 1.85; 95% CI: 1.49–2.29). [10.1111/ggi.70858].
  • Compared to the HBV-DNA-negative group, HBV-DNA-positivity was significantly associated with aggravated hepatic injury, as evidenced by elevated ALT, AST, lymphocyte count, and fibrosis scores (APRI and FIB-4).

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Clinical infectious diseases : an official publication of the Infectious Diseases Society of America  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-29  |  DOI: 10.1093/cid/ciag585  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, ciag585

美国肝移植受者的乙型肝炎 疫苗接种:一项国家 队列研究 研究。

Hepatitis B Vaccination for Liver Transplant Recipients in the United States: A National Cohort Study.
作者： Michael A. Shi, Jeong W. Han, Ramsey Albahra, Maria Fernanda Machicao, Chia-Yu Chiu, Saira Aijaz Khaderi, Ruben Hernaez, Fasiha Kanwal, Hashem B El-Serag, George T. Cholankeril
摘要中文翻译根据器官共享联合网络数据库(9/1/2022-6/30/2024),只有59.9%的肝移植受者接种了乙型肝炎病毒疫苗。疫苗接种 率因地区和其他临床人口统计学因素而异。多层次干预和使用更具免疫原性的 疫苗 可能会提高该人群的 疫苗接种 率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决肝移植受者中 乙型肝炎病毒 的低 疫苗接种 比率问题,强调需要改进策略来预防这一高危人群中的 HBV 感染。
设计与对象
使用联合器官共享网络数据库进行了一项全国性 队列研究 研究,涵盖 2022 年 9 月 1 日至 2024 年 6 月 30 日的肝移植受者,重点关注 疫苗接种 状况和地区差异。
核心方法
该研究利用器官共享联合网络数据库的数据来分析 疫苗接种 率,重点是确定影响区域差异的因素并提出干预措施。
主要结果
只有 59.9% 的肝移植受者接种了 乙型肝炎病毒 疫苗,不同地区和临床人口统计学因素之间存在显着差异。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,目前的 疫苗接种 做法还不够,可能需要有针对性的干预措施来提高肝移植受者的覆盖率。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了提高肝移植受者的 疫苗接种 率对于降低 HBV 感染及其相关并发症的风险的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖单一数据库而受到限制,该数据库可能无法捕获所有相关的临床人口统计学因素,并且由于这些限制,证据被认为是中等的。

Hepatitis B Vaccination for Liver Transplant Recipients in the United States: A National Cohort Study.

Authors: Michael A. Shi, Jeong W. Han, Ramsey Albahra, Maria Fernanda Machicao, Chia-Yu Chiu, Saira Aijaz Khaderi, Ruben Hernaez, Fasiha Kanwal, Hashem B El-Serag, George T. Cholankeril
Original abstract (English)
Based on the United Network for Organ Sharing database (9/1/2022-6/30/2024), only 59.9% liver transplant recipients were vaccinated against hepatitis B virus. The vaccination rate varied widely by region and other clinicodemographic factors. Multilevel interventions and the utilization of a more immunogenic vaccine may improve vaccination rates in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the low vaccination rates among liver transplant recipients against hepatitis B virus, highlighting the need for improved strategies to prevent HBV infection in this high-risk population.
Design and population
A national cohort study was conducted using the United Network for Organ Sharing database, covering liver transplant recipients from 9/1/2022 to 6/30/2024, with a focus on vaccination status and regional variations.
Core methods
The study utilized data from the United Network for Organ Sharing database to analyze vaccination rates, with an emphasis on identifying factors influencing regional disparities and suggesting interventions.
Main results
Only 59.9% of liver transplant recipients were vaccinated against hepatitis B virus, with significant variation observed across different regions and clinicodemographic factors.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that current vaccination practices are insufficient, and targeted interventions may be necessary to improve coverage in liver transplant recipients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results emphasize the importance of improving vaccination rates in liver transplant recipients to reduce the risk of HBV infection and its associated complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a single database, which may not capture all relevant clinicodemographic factors, and the evidence is considered moderate due to these limitations.

Journal: Vaccine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-27  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.vaccine.2026.129183  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 93, 129183

评估六联疫苗在接种六联疫苗的成年造血干细胞移植受者中乙型肝炎病毒疫苗接种的免疫原性与接种联合乙型肝炎病毒和甲型肝炎病毒疫苗的患者相比

Evaluation of the immunogenicity of hepatitis B vaccination in hematopoietic stem cell transplant recipients vaccinated with a hexavalent vaccine compared with patients vaccinated with a combined hepatitis A and B vaccine
作者： Laura Marina Montes Camiño, Marta Aldea, Carmen Martínez, Alberto L. García-Basteiro, Guillermo Mena, Carmen Martı́nez, Alberto L. García‐Basteiro
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在比较两种乙型肝炎病毒疫苗接种疫苗接种策略在成年HSCT受者中的免疫原性,并确定免疫应答的预测因素。背景强调了在这一人群中明确六联疫苗疫苗效果的必要性。
设计与对象
在巴塞罗那一家三级医院中,进行了一项回顾性队列研究研究,纳入了2017年至2023年间接种疫苗的成年HSCT受者。患者接种了联合甲型肝炎病毒和乙型肝炎病毒疫苗疫苗或六联疫苗疫苗。共纳入了331名患者。
核心方法
血清保护定义为在第三剂疫苗接种后一个月内HBsAb ≥10 IU/mL。使用单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析确定与血清应答相关的因素。研究比较了两种疫苗接种疫苗接种策略在成年HSCT受者中的效果。
主要结果
Seroprotection rates were higher with the hexavalent vaccine compared to hepatitis A + B (70.1% vs. Median HBsAb titers were higher but not statistically significant (668 vs. 375 IU/mL; p = 0.261) in the hexavalent group. In multivariate analysis, hexavalent vaccination (OR 4.27, 95% CI 2.88-6.36) and pre-HSCT seroprotection (OR 9.36, 95% CI 5.46-16.03) were associated with higher response. Tacrolimus use and multiple myeloma were associated with lower response.
解释与创新
作者解释称,尽管六联疫苗疫苗的抗原含量较低,但其在成年HSCT受者中与显著更高的血清保护相关。他们确定了既往保护性HBsAb滴度作为免疫应答的预测因素,同时指出他克莫司暴露和多发性骨髓瘤与免疫原性降低相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为成年HSCT受者的疫苗接种策略提供了信息,突出了六联疫苗疫苗的潜在优势,并强调了既往免疫力和免疫抑制治疗对疫苗应答的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到回顾性设计和潜在混杂因素的限制。证据强度中等,因为结果基于观察性数据,而非随机对照试验。

Evaluation of the immunogenicity of hepatitis B vaccination in hematopoietic stem cell transplant recipients vaccinated with a hexavalent vaccine compared with patients vaccinated with a combined hepatitis A and B vaccine

Authors: Laura Marina Montes Camiño, Marta Aldea, Carmen Martínez, Alberto L. García-Basteiro, Guillermo Mena, Carmen Martı́nez, Alberto L. García‐Basteiro
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: In 2020, the Catalan Department of Health recommended a pediatric hexavalent vaccine for adult hematopoietic stem cell transplant (HSCT) recipients. Previously, patients at our center received a combined hepatitis A + B vaccine with double the hepatitis B antigen content. The immunogenicity of the hexavalent formulation in this population remains unclear. OBJECTIVE: To compare immunogenicity of two hepatitis B vaccination strategies in adult HSCT recipients and identify predictors of response. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective cohort study including adult HSCT recipients vaccinated between 2017 and 2023 in a tertiary hospital in Barcelona. Patients received hepatitis A + B vaccine (2017-2019) or hexavalent vaccine (2020-2023). Seroprotection was defined as HBsAb ≥10 IU/mL one month after the third dose. Univariate and multivariate logistic regression were used to identify factors associated with seroresponse. RESULTS: A total of 331 patients were included (median age 54.6 years; 42.3% female). Seroprotection rates were higher with the hexavalent vaccine compared to hepatitis A + B (70.1% vs. Median HBsAb titers were higher but not statistically significant (668 vs. 375 IU/mL; p = 0.261) in the hexavalent group. In multivariate analysis, hexavalent vaccination (OR 4.27, 95% CI 2.88-6.36) and pre-HSCT seroprotection (OR 9.36, 95% CI 5.46-16.03) were associated with higher response. Tacrolimus use and multiple myeloma were associated with lower response. CONCLUSIONS: Despite lower antigen content, the hexavalent vaccine was associated with significantly higher seroprotection in adult HSCT recipients. Pre-existing protective HBsAb titers enhanced response, whereas tacrolimus exposure and multiple myeloma were associated with reduced immunogenicity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare immunogenicity of two hepatitis B vaccination strategies in adult HSCT recipients and identify predictors of response. The background highlighted the need for clarity on the effectiveness of the hexavalent vaccine in this population.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted including adult HSCT recipients vaccinated between 2017 and 2023 in a tertiary hospital in Barcelona. Patients received either a combined hepatitis A + B vaccine or a hexavalent vaccine. A total of 331 patients were included in the study.
Core methods
Seroprotection was defined as HBsAb ≥10 IU/mL one month after the third dose. Univariate and multivariate logistic regression were used to identify factors associated with seroresponse. The study compared two vaccination strategies in adult HSCT recipients.
Main results
Seroprotection rates were higher with the hexavalent vaccine compared to hepatitis A + B (70.1% vs. Median HBsAb titers were higher but not statistically significant (668 vs. 375 IU/mL; p = 0.261) in the hexavalent group. In multivariate analysis, hexavalent vaccination (OR 4.27, 95% CI 2.88-6.36) and pre-HSCT seroprotection (OR 9.36, 95% CI 5.46-16.03) were associated with higher response. Tacrolimus use and multiple myeloma were associated with lower response.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the hexavalent vaccine was associated with significantly higher seroprotection in adult HSCT recipients despite lower antigen content. They identified pre-existing protective HBsAb titers as a predictor of response, while tacrolimus exposure and multiple myeloma were linked to reduced immunogenicity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform vaccination strategies for adult HSCT recipients, highlighting the potential benefits of the hexavalent vaccine and the impact of pre-existing immunity and immunosuppressive therapies on vaccine response.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for confounding factors. The evidence is moderate, as the results are based on observational data and not randomized controlled trials.

一种基于马尔可夫模型的潜在乙型肝炎病毒治愈方案成本阈值分析

A Markov model cost threshold analysis of potential hepatitis B virus cure regimens
作者： Grace Lai-Hung Wong, Robert John Wong, Bo-Yu Yang, Vincent Wai-Sun Wong, Jimmy Che-To Lai, Henry Lik-Yuen Chan, Hong-Lian Bai, Hong-Zhou Lu, Xiao-Ping Tang, Jian Sun
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
新型治疗方案以实现功能性治愈(功能性治愈)的慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)可能即将问世。本研究旨在利用马尔可夫模型,评估治疗在美中两国潜在治愈方案中的成本效益阈值,以长期核苷(酸)类似物(NUC)治疗作为参考。
设计与对象
该研究模拟了疾病进展,比较了以HBsAg消失和HBV DNA不可量化作为功能性治愈(FC)标准的治疗方案在24周后与新型治疗方案的效果,以及与长期使用NUC治疗的对比。基线案例为一名50岁的男性,患有乙型肝炎e抗原(HBeAg)阴性、非肝硬化的慢性乙型肝炎(CHB),且HBV DNA已被NUC抑制≥1年。
核心方法
功能性治愈慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)的成本效果阈值,结合了功能性治愈(FC)的各种预期成功率、功能性治愈后主要肝脏结局(包括肝细胞癌)的发生率、健康效用值以及医疗成本,通过马尔可夫模型进行了估算。
主要结果
一种30%有效的FC方案在美国和中国分别可获得17.18和16.14个质量调整生命年(QALYs),每位患者分别获得0.30和0.57个QALYs的增益。成本效果阈值随着更高的FC率、更年轻的患者和医疗成本的增加而上升,但在女性中可能较低。
解释与创新
A Markov model-based cost-effectiveness analysis demonstrates that CHB cure regimen with 30% FC in aforementioned base case could be cost-effective at a threshold of USD46,642 in the U.S. and USD13,008 in China.
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为美国和中国潜在的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)治愈方案的成本效益决策提供信息,特别是针对HBeAg阴性、非肝硬化的慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者以及接受核苷(酸)类似物(NUC)治疗且HBV DNA被抑制≥1年的患者。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于其依赖预期成功率和发病率率来评估肝病结果,这些数据可能无法反映真实世界的情况。由于其基于模拟数据和假设,证据强度为中等。

A Markov model cost threshold analysis of potential hepatitis B virus cure regimens

Authors: Grace Lai-Hung Wong, Robert John Wong, Bo-Yu Yang, Vincent Wai-Sun Wong, Jimmy Che-To Lai, Henry Lik-Yuen Chan, Hong-Lian Bai, Hong-Zhou Lu, Xiao-Ping Tang, Jian Sun
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Novel treatment regimens to achieve functional cure (FC) of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) may soon be available. We aimed to evaluate treatment cost-effectiveness thresholds for a potential cure regimen in the U.S. and China using a Markov model, with long-term nucleos(t)ide analogue (NUC) treatment as reference. METHODS: We simulated disease progression comparing achieving FC defined as HBsAg loss and unquantifiable HBV DNA 24 weeks off treatment with a novel therapeutic regimen to long-term NUC treatment. Cost-effectiveness thresholds for FC of CHB, incorporating various anticipated success rates for FC, incidence rates of major liver outcomes including hepatocellular carcinoma post-FC, health utility values, and medical costs, were estimated. RESULTS: The base case was a 50-year-old man with hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg)-negative non-cirrhotic CHB and NUC-suppressed HBV DNA ≥1year. Compared to long-term NUC, a 30% effective FC regimen would yield 17.18 and 16.14 quality-adjusted life years (QALYs), and 0.30 and 0.57 QALYs gained per patient in the U.S. and China, respectively. With an HCC annual incidence rate of 0.1% after FC, a FC regimen could be cost-effective at a one-time treatment cost under the threshold of USD46,642 in the U.S., and USD13,008 in China. The cost-effectiveness threshold increases with higher FC rates, younger patients, and increasing medical costs, but may be lower in women. DISCUSSION: A Markov model-based cost-effectiveness analysis demonstrates that CHB cure regimen with 30% FC in aforementioned base case could be cost-effective at a threshold of USD46,642 in the U.S. and USD13,008 in China.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Novel treatment regimens to achieve functional cure (FC) of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) may soon be available. The study aimed to evaluate treatment cost-effectiveness thresholds for a potential cure regimen in the U.S. and China using a Markov model, with long-term nucleos(t)ide analogue (NUC) treatment as reference.
Design and population
The study simulated disease progression comparing achieving FC defined as HBsAg loss and unquantifiable HBV DNA 24 weeks off treatment with a novel therapeutic regimen to long-term NUC treatment. The base case was a 50-year-old man with hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg)-negative non-cirrhotic CHB and NUC-suppressed HBV DNA ≥1 year.
Core methods
Cost-effectiveness thresholds for FC of CHB, incorporating various anticipated success rates for FC, incidence rates of major liver outcomes including hepatocellular carcinoma post-FC, health utility values, and medical costs, were estimated using a Markov model.
Main results
A 30% effective FC regimen would yield 17.18 and 16.14 quality-adjusted life years (QALYs), and 0.30 and 0.57 QALYs gained per patient in the U.S. and China, respectively. The cost-effectiveness threshold increases with higher FC rates, younger patients, and increasing medical costs, but may be lower in women.
Interpretation and novelty
A Markov model-based cost-effectiveness analysis demonstrates that CHB cure regimen with 30% FC in aforementioned base case could be cost-effective at a threshold of USD46,642 in the U.S. and USD13,008 in China.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform cost-effectiveness decisions for potential HBV cure regimens in the U.S. and China, particularly for patients with HBeAg-negative non-cirrhotic CHB and NUC-suppressed HBV DNA ≥1 year.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on anticipated success rates and incidence rates for liver outcomes, which may not reflect real-world scenarios. The evidence strength is moderate as it is based on simulated data and assumptions.

Journal: International Journal of Hematology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-27  |  DOI: 10.1007/s12185-026-04292-1  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

解决的乙型肝炎病毒感染对弥漫性大B细胞淋巴瘤患者临床结果的影响:JCOG0601的补充分析

Impact of resolved hepatitis B virus infection on clinical outcomes in patients with diffuse large B-cell lymphoma: a supplementary analysis of JCOG0601
作者： Shinya Hagiwara, Shigeru Kusumoto, Ryunosuke Machida, Ken Ohmachi, Junya Makiyama, Wataru Munakata, Kiyoshi Ando, Tomohiro Kinoshita, Kunihiro Tsukasaki, Hirokazu Nagai
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
解决的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染,定义为治疗前乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)阴性且乙型肝炎核心抗原抗体(anti-HBc)阳性,对弥漫性大B细胞淋巴瘤(DLBCL)的临床特征和结果的影响仍不清楚。
设计与对象
JCOG0601 was a randomized phase II/III trial showing no improvement in progression-free survival (PFS) with dose-dense rituximab plus CHOP in previously untreated DLBCL. Among 406 eligible patients randomly assigned in JCOG0601, 405 with available baseline HBV serologic data were included. Of the 405 patients, 87 were anti-HBc-positive and 318 were anti-HBc-negative.
核心方法
根据anti-HBc状态比较基线特征和结果。
主要结果
Anti-HBc-positive patients were older than anti-HBc-negative patients (median age, 63 vs. 61 years; p = 0.008). No significant differences in PFS or OS were observed between the two groups (3-year PFS, 78% vs. 81%; 3-year OS, 90% vs. HBV reactivation occurred in five anti-HBc-positive patients.
解释与创新
基线anti-HBc阳性在老年患者中更为常见,但并未与接受含利妥昔单抗的CHOP方案治疗的HBsAg阴性DLBCL患者的较差结果相关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,解决的HBV感染不会对接受含利妥昔单抗的CHOP方案治疗的HBsAg阴性DLBCL患者的临床结果产生负面影响。
局限与证据强度
本研究是对一项随机试验的补充分析,这可能限制结论的深度。由于分析是在试验框架内的观察性研究,证据强度中等。

Impact of resolved hepatitis B virus infection on clinical outcomes in patients with diffuse large B-cell lymphoma: a supplementary analysis of JCOG0601

Authors: Shinya Hagiwara, Shigeru Kusumoto, Ryunosuke Machida, Ken Ohmachi, Junya Makiyama, Wataru Munakata, Kiyoshi Ando, Tomohiro Kinoshita, Kunihiro Tsukasaki, Hirokazu Nagai
Original abstract (English)
The impact of resolved hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, defined as pretreatment hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) negativity with antibody to hepatitis B core antigen (anti-HBc) positivity, on the clinical characteristics and outcomes of diffuse large B-cell lymphoma (DLBCL) remains unclear. JCOG0601 was a randomized phase II/III trial showing no improvement in progression-free survival (PFS) with dose-dense rituximab plus CHOP in previously untreated DLBCL. This supplementary analysis of JCOG0601 evaluated whether baseline anti-HBc positivity affects PFS and overall survival (OS) in patients with HBsAg-negative DLBCL treated with rituximab-containing CHOP-based therapy. Among 406 eligible patients randomly assigned in JCOG0601, 405 with available baseline HBV serologic data were included. Baseline characteristics and outcomes were compared according to anti-HBc status. Of the 405 patients, 87 were anti-HBc-positive and 318 were anti-HBc-negative. Anti-HBc-positive patients were older than anti-HBc-negative patients (median age, 63 vs. 61 years; p = 0.008). No significant differences in PFS or OS were observed between the two groups (3-year PFS, 78% vs. 81%; 3-year OS, 90% vs. HBV reactivation occurred in five anti-HBc-positive patients. Baseline anti-HBc positivity was more common in older patients but was not associated with inferior outcomes in HBsAg-negative DLBCL treated with rituximab-containing CHOP-based therapy.Clinical trial registration: JCOG0601 was registered in the Japan Registry of Clinical Trials (jRCTs031180139, date of registration; February 20, 2019).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The impact of resolved hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, defined as pretreatment hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) negativity with antibody to hepatitis B core antigen (anti-HBc) positivity, on the clinical characteristics and outcomes of diffuse large B-cell lymphoma (DLBCL) remains unclear.
Design and population
JCOG0601 was a randomized phase II/III trial showing no improvement in progression-free survival (PFS) with dose-dense rituximab plus CHOP in previously untreated DLBCL. Among 406 eligible patients randomly assigned in JCOG0601, 405 with available baseline HBV serologic data were included. Of the 405 patients, 87 were anti-HBc-positive and 318 were anti-HBc-negative.
Core methods
Baseline characteristics and outcomes were compared according to anti-HBc status.
Main results
Anti-HBc-positive patients were older than anti-HBc-negative patients (median age, 63 vs. 61 years; p = 0.008). No significant differences in PFS or OS were observed between the two groups (3-year PFS, 78% vs. 81%; 3-year OS, 90% vs. HBV reactivation occurred in five anti-HBc-positive patients.
Interpretation and novelty
Baseline anti-HBc positivity was more common in older patients but was not associated with inferior outcomes in HBsAg-negative DLBCL treated with rituximab-containing CHOP-based therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that resolved HBV infection does not negatively impact clinical outcomes in patients with HBsAg-negative DLBCL receiving rituximab-containing CHOP-based therapy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a supplementary analysis of a randomized trial, which may limit the depth of conclusions. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the analysis within a trial framework.

白细胞与高密度脂蛋白胆固醇比值与肌少症:一项双中心分析及对乙型肝炎病毒史的探索性分析

White Blood Cell to HDL ‐C Ratio and Sarcopenia: A Dual‐Cohort Analysis Including Exploratory Insights on Hepatitis B History
作者： Wen-Shuo Cheng, Hui-Ling Xiang, 成文碩
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 肌少症由炎症和脂质调节紊乱驱动,缺乏经济实惠的筛查工具。白细胞与高密度脂蛋白胆固醇比值(WHR)整合了这些通路,但其在肌少症筛查中的作用尚未得到验证。目的 本研究评估了WHR与肌少症之间的关联,通过双中心分析进行验证,并探讨了乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)状态对这种关联的潜在效应修饰。方法 我们分析了10131名NHANES(2011–2018)和7872名KNHANES(2010–2011)成年人的数据。根据FNIH/AWGS标准,通过双能X射线吸收法(DXA)定义肌少症。应用加权逻辑回归、受限立方样条和机器学习(LASSO、XGBoost)方法。结果 NHANES中较高的ln(WHR)与肌少症独立相关(调整后OR = 2.50;95% CI:2.01–3.10),并在KNHANES中一致验证(OR = 1.85;95% CI:1.49–2.29)。WHR在机器学习模型中位列预测特征的前列。探索性分层分析表明,ln(WHR)与肌少症之间的关联在乙型肝炎病毒暴露亚组中似乎更强。然而,尽管正式的交互作用检验不显著(p = 0.270),这些发现仍具有生成假设的意义。结论 WHR是一种无成本的常规指标,与肌少症有强关联。将其纳入老年评估可有助于早期识别高风险个体,从而及时干预。然而,探索性结果提示乙型肝炎病毒史可能具有潜在的修饰作用,需要前瞻性验证。

问题与背景
肌少症由炎症和脂质调节紊乱驱动,缺乏经济实惠的筛查工具。白细胞与高密度脂蛋白胆固醇比值(WHR)整合了这些通路,但其在肌少症筛查中的作用尚未得到验证。
设计与对象
本研究评估了WHR与肌少症之间的关联,通过双中心分析进行验证,并探讨了乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)状态对这种关联的潜在效应修饰。我们分析了10131名NHANES(2011–2018)和7872名KNHANES(2010–2011)成年人的数据。

WHR是一种无成本的常规指标,与肌少症有强关联。将其纳入老年评估可有助于早期识别高风险个体,从而及时干预。然而,探索性结果提示乙型肝炎病毒史可能具有潜在的修饰作用,需要前瞻性验证。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未明确报告与研究设计或统计方法相关的局限性。由于HBV相关发现的探索性性质以及交互作用检验结果不显著,证据强度中等。

White Blood Cell to HDL ‐C Ratio and Sarcopenia: A Dual‐Cohort Analysis Including Exploratory Insights on Hepatitis B History

Authors: Wen-Shuo Cheng, Hui-Ling Xiang, 成文碩
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Sarcopenia, driven by inflammation and lipid dysregulation, lacks inexpensive screening tools. The white blood cell to HDL‐C ratio (WHR) integrates these pathways, but its utility for sarcopenia screening remains unvalidated. Objective This study evaluated the WHR‐sarcopenia association in dual cohorts, validated it using machine learning, and explored potential effect modification of hepatitis B virus (HBV) status. Methods We analyzed 10 131 NHANES (2011–2018) and 7872 KNHANES (2010–2011) adults. Sarcopenia was defined via DXA using FNIH/AWGS criteria. Weighted logistic regression, restricted cubic splines, and machine learning (LASSO, XGBoost) were applied. Results Higher ln(WHR) was independently associated with sarcopenia in NHANES (adjusted OR = 2.50; 95% CI: 2.01–3.10) and consistently validated in KNHANES (OR = 1.85; 95% CI: 1.49–2.29). WHR ranked among the top predictive features across machine learning models. Exploratory stratified analysis suggests that the association between ln(WHR) and sarcopenia appears to be stronger in the HBV‐exposed subgroup. However, although the formal interaction test was not significant ( p = 0.270), these findings are hypothesis‐generating results. Conclusion WHR, a cost‐free routine metric, is robustly linked to sarcopenia. Integrating it into geriatric assessments could facilitate early high‐risk identification for timely interventions, while exploratory findings suggesting a possible modifying role of HBV history require prospective validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Sarcopenia, driven by inflammation and lipid dysregulation, lacks inexpensive screening tools. The white blood cell to HDL‐C ratio (WHR) integrates these pathways, but its utility for sarcopenia screening remains unvalidated.
Design and population
This study evaluated the WHR‐sarcopenia association in dual cohorts, validated it using machine learning, and explored potential effect modification of hepatitis B virus (HBV) status. We analyzed 10 131 NHANES (2011–2018) and 7872 KNHANES (2010–2011) adults.
Core methods
Sarcopenia was defined via DXA using FNIH/AWGS criteria. Weighted logistic regression, restricted cubic splines, and machine learning (LASSO, XGBoost) were applied. WHR ranked among the top predictive features across machine learning models.
Main results
Higher ln(WHR) was independently associated with sarcopenia in NHANES (adjusted OR = 2.50; 95% CI: 2.01–3.10) and consistently validated in KNHANES (OR = 1.85; 95% CI: 1.49–2.29).
Interpretation and novelty
Exploratory stratified analysis suggests that the association between ln(WHR) and sarcopenia appears to be stronger in the HBV‐exposed subgroup. However, although the formal interaction test was not significant ( p = 0.270), these findings are hypothesis‐generating results.
Scientific and public-health significance
WHR, a cost‐free routine metric, is robustly linked to sarcopenia. Integrating it into geriatric assessments could facilitate early high‐risk identification for timely interventions, while exploratory findings suggesting a possible modifying role of HBV history require prospective validation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report explicit limitations related to the study design or statistical methods. The evidence strength is moderate due to the exploratory nature of the HBV-related findings and the lack of significant interaction test results.

细胞内Zn2+水平通过调节NTCP介导的病毒入侵来调控对乙型肝炎病毒感染的易感性。

Intracellular Zn2+ levels regulate susceptibility to hepatitis B virus infection by modulating NTCP-mediated viral entry.
作者： Yumi Itoh, Chiharu Morita, Cui Jinzi, Tatsuya Suzuki, Aiko Ono, Tomomi Hishinuma, Kento Fukano, Daisuke Okuzaki, Daisuke Motooka, Kei Miyakawa
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染仍然是一个重大的全球健康挑战,是包括肝硬化和肝细胞癌在内的慢性肝病的主要病因。尽管核苷(酸)类似物和基于干扰素的治疗可以抑制病毒复制,但它们并不能清除病毒,这凸显了开发新型治疗策略的必要性。HBV利用钠胆酸共转运蛋白(NTCP)作为肝细胞进入的关键受体;然而,调控这一过程的潜在机制仍不完全清楚。在这项研究中,我们证明了细胞内锌离子(Zn 2+)水平,由锌转运蛋白1(ZnT1),也称为溶质载体家族30成员1(SLC30A1)所调控,是HBV进入的重要调节因子。补充锌显著降低了在表达NTCP的肝细胞中HBV和肝炎D病毒的感染,而不会影响进入后的复制步骤。敲除或敲减SLC30A1导致细胞内锌离子积累并损害HBV感染,这些效应可通过重新表达功能性SLC30A1或治疗与锌离子螯合剂TPEN逆转。从机制上看,锌离子水平升高破坏了NTCP介导的HBV/HDV内化和胆汁酸摄取,表明锌离子在调控受体活性中起作用。RNA测序分析进一步揭示了在锌离子处理的细胞中,代谢和胆汁酸相关通路的转录重编程。综上所述,这些发现表明细胞内锌稳态在调控HBV进入中起作用,并提示SLC30A1-Zn2+-NTCP轴参与这一过程。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染仍然是全球重要的健康挑战,并是导致慢性肝病(包括肝硬化和肝细胞癌)的主要原因。尽管核苷(酸)类似物和基于干扰素的治疗可以抑制病毒复制,但它们无法清除病毒储存宿主,凸显了开发新型治疗策略的必要性。
设计与对象
本研究旨在通过调节NTCP介导的病毒入侵来探讨细胞内Zn2+水平在调控HBV入侵中的作用。研究使用了表达NTCP的肝细胞,并涉及RNA测序分析。
核心方法
本研究利用RNA测序分析了Zn2+处理细胞中的代谢和胆汁酸相关通路。此外,还通过敲除或敲减SLC30A1以及使用Zn2+螯合剂TPEN结合治疗来评估其对HBV感染的影响。
主要结果
补充锌显著降低了NTCP表达肝细胞中HBV和肝炎D病毒的感染,而不会影响入侵后的复制步骤。机制上,Zn2+水平升高破坏了NTCP介导的HBV/HDV内化和胆汁酸摄取,表明Zn2+在调节受体活性中起作用。
解释与创新
敲除或敲减SLC30A1导致细胞内Zn2+积累并损害HBV感染,这些效应可通过重新表达功能性SLC30A1或与Zn2+螯合剂TPEN结合的治疗逆转。这些发现表明细胞内锌稳态在调控HBV入侵中起作用,并暗示了SLC30A1-Zn2+-NTCP轴在此过程中的作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,针对细胞内Zn2+水平可能是一种治疗HBV感染的新策略。这可能对开发新的治疗并改善患者预后产生影响。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于使用了体外模型,这可能无法完全反映体内条件。此外,样本量和特定宿主人群未详细说明,这可能影响结果的普遍性。证据强度为中等。

Intracellular Zn2+ levels regulate susceptibility to hepatitis B virus infection by modulating NTCP-mediated viral entry.

Authors: Yumi Itoh, Chiharu Morita, Cui Jinzi, Tatsuya Suzuki, Aiko Ono, Tomomi Hishinuma, Kento Fukano, Daisuke Okuzaki, Daisuke Motooka, Kei Miyakawa
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a significant global health challenge and is a leading cause of chronic liver diseases, including cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. Although nucleos(t)ide analogs and interferon-based therapies suppress viral replication, they do not eliminate the viral reservoir, highlighting the need for novel therapeutic strategies. HBV utilizes the sodium taurocholate cotransporting polypeptide (NTCP) as a key receptor for hepatocyte entry; however, the underlying mechanisms governing this process remain incompletely understood. In this study, we demonstrate that intracellular Zn 2+ levels, regulated by zinc transporter 1 (ZnT1), also known as solute carrier family 30 member 1 (SLC30A1), serve as critical modulators of HBV entry. Zinc supplementation significantly reduced both HBV and hepatitis D virus infection in NTCP-expressing hepatocytes without affecting post-entry replication steps. Knockout or knockdown of SLC30A1 led to intracellular Zn 2+ accumulation and impaired HBV infection, effects reversed by re-expression of functional SLC30A1 or treatment with the Zn 2+ chelator TPEN. Mechanistically, elevated Zn 2+ levels disrupted NTCP-mediated HBV/HDV internalization and bile acid uptake, implicating Zn 2+ in the regulation of receptor activity. RNA sequencing analysis further revealed transcriptional reprogramming of metabolic and bile acid-associated pathways in Zn 2+ -treated cells. Collectively, these findings reveal a role for intracellular zinc homeostasis in regulating HBV entry and implicate the SLC30A1-Zn2+-NTCP axis in this process.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a significant global health challenge and is a leading cause of chronic liver diseases, including cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. Although nucleos(t)ide analogs and interferon-based therapies suppress viral replication, they do not eliminate the viral reservoir, highlighting the need for novel therapeutic strategies.
Design and population
This study aimed to investigate the role of intracellular Zn2+ levels in regulating HBV entry by modulating NTCP-mediated viral entry. The research was conducted using NTCP-expressing hepatocytes and involved RNA sequencing analysis.
Core methods
The study utilized RNA sequencing to analyze metabolic and bile acid-associated pathways in Zn2+-treated cells. Additionally, knockout or knockdown of SLC30A1 and treatment with the Zn2+ chelator TPEN were employed to assess the effects on HBV infection.
Main results
Zinc supplementation significantly reduced both HBV and hepatitis D virus infection in NTCP-expressing hepatocytes without affecting post-entry replication steps. Mechanistically, elevated Zn2+ levels disrupted NTCP-mediated HBV/HDV internalization and bile acid uptake, implicating Zn2+ in the regulation of receptor activity.
Interpretation and novelty
Knockout or knockdown of SLC30A1 led to intracellular Zn2+ accumulation and impaired HBV infection, effects reversed by re-expression of functional SLC30A1 or treatment with the Zn2+ chelator TPEN. These findings reveal a role for intracellular zinc homeostasis in regulating HBV entry and implicate the SLC30A1-Zn2+-NTCP axis in this process.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting intracellular Zn2+ levels could be a novel therapeutic strategy for HBV infection. This could have implications for the development of new treatments and improve patient outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of in vitro models, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. Additionally, the sample size and specific host populations were not detailed, which may affect generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate.

EBV溶性复制与HBeAg阴性慢性HBV感染患者肝损伤之间的关联

Association Between EBV Lytic Replication and Liver Injury in Patients With HBeAg‐Negative Chronic HBV Infection
作者： Yong-Xi Zhang, Li-Ping Deng, Rong-Rong Yang, Yong Xiong, Xien Gui, Ke Lan, Ke Zhuang, 杨蓉蓉, 桂希恩
摘要中文翻译摘要 本研究旨在探讨Epstein-Barr病毒(EBV)溶性复制对乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染患者肝病的影响。我们回顾性纳入了2019年至2025年间在中南医院就诊的261例HBeAg阴性慢性HBV感染患者。收集了患者的血浆HBV-DNA、EBV-DNA水平以及肝功能参数。根据病毒DNA状态将患者分组,并评估病毒DNA状态与肝功能之间的关联。与HBV-DNA阴性组相比,HBV-DNA阳性与肝损伤加重显著相关,表现为ALT、AST升高,淋巴细胞计数增加以及纤维化评分(APRI和FIB-4)升高。值得注意的是,无论HBV-DNA状态如何或肝病阶段(慢性乙型肝炎或肝硬化),EBV-DNA阳性均与血浆白蛋白(Alb)水平和白蛋白-球蛋白(A/G)比值显著降低相关,与EBV-DNA阴性患者相比。A/G比值的降低归因于白蛋白水平的下降,而非球蛋白(Glb)水平的升高。这些结果表明,在本研究中,EBV溶性复制与低白蛋白血症相关。循环EBV-DNA状态可能作为监测慢性HBV感染患者疾病进展或评估疾病严重程度的潜在生物标志物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨Epstein-Barr病毒(EBV)溶性复制对乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染患者肝病的影响。
设计与对象
本研究回顾性分析了2019年至2025年间在中南医院就诊的261例HBeAg阴性慢性HBV感染患者。根据病毒DNA状态将患者分组,并评估病毒DNA状态与肝功能之间的关联。
核心方法
收集了患者的血浆HBV-DNA、EBV-DNA水平以及肝功能参数。通过分组并评估两者之间的关系,分析病毒DNA状态与肝功能的关联。
主要结果
与HBV-DNA阴性组相比,HBV-DNA阳性与肝损伤加重显著相关,表现为ALT、AST升高,淋巴细胞计数增加以及纤维化评分(APRI和FIB-4)升高。无论HBV-DNA状态如何或肝病阶段(慢性乙型肝炎或肝硬化),EBV-DNA阳性均与血浆白蛋白(Alb)水平和白蛋白-球蛋白(A/G)比值显著降低相关,与EBV-DNA阴性患者相比。A/G比值的降低归因于白蛋白水平的下降,而非球蛋白(Glb)水平的升高。
解释与创新
这些结果表明,在本研究中,EBV溶性复制与低白蛋白血症相关。
科研与公卫意义
循环EBV-DNA状态可能作为监测慢性HBV感染患者疾病进展或评估疾病严重程度的潜在生物标志物。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其回顾性设计和潜在的混杂因素,但证据较强,因为其基于病毒DNA和肝功能参数的直接测量。

Association Between EBV Lytic Replication and Liver Injury in Patients With HBeAg‐Negative Chronic HBV Infection

Authors: Yong-Xi Zhang, Li-Ping Deng, Rong-Rong Yang, Yong Xiong, Xien Gui, Ke Lan, Ke Zhuang, 杨蓉蓉, 桂希恩
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The present study aims to characterize the impact of Epstein‐Barr virus (EBV) lytic replication on liver disease in patients with chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection. We retrospectively enrolled 261 patients with HBeAg‐negative chronic HBV infection who were admitted to Zhongnan Hospital between 2019 and 2025. Plasma levels of HBV‐DNA, EBV‐DNA, and liver function parameters were collected. Patients were grouped based on their viral DNA status, and the association between viral DNA status and liver function was evaluated. Compared to the HBV‐DNA‐negative group, HBV‐DNA‐positivity was significantly associated with aggravated hepatic injury, as evidenced by elevated ALT, AST, lymphocyte count, and fibrosis scores (APRI and FIB‐4). Notably, regardless of HBV‐DNA status or the stage of liver disease (CHB or LC), EBV‐DNA‐positivity was consistently associated with significantly lower plasma albumin (Alb) levels and albumin‐to‐globulin (A/G) ratio compared to EBV‐DNA‐negative patients. The reduction in the A/G ratio was attributable to decreased Alb levels rather than to elevated globulin (Glb) levels. These results suggest that EBV lytic replication was associated with hypoalbuminemia in this study. Circulating EBV‐DNA status holds promise as a potential biomarker for monitoring disease progression, or assessing disease severity in patients with chronic HBV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the impact of Epstein-Barr virus (EBV) lytic replication on liver disease in patients with chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection.
Design and population
A retrospective study was conducted on 261 patients with HBeAg-negative chronic HBV infection admitted to Zhongnan Hospital between 2019 and 2025. Patients were grouped based on their viral DNA status, and the association between viral DNA status and liver function was evaluated.
Core methods
Plasma levels of HBV-DNA, EBV-DNA, and liver function parameters were collected. The association between viral DNA status and liver function was assessed through grouping patients and evaluating the relationship.
Main results
Compared to the HBV-DNA-negative group, HBV-DNA-positivity was significantly associated with aggravated hepatic injury, as evidenced by elevated ALT, AST, lymphocyte count, and fibrosis scores (APRI and FIB-4). Regardless of HBV-DNA status or the stage of liver disease (CHB or LC), EBV-DNA-positivity was consistently associated with significantly lower plasma albumin (Alb) levels and albumin-to-globulin (A/G) ratio compared to EBV-DNA-negative patients. The reduction in the A/G ratio was attributable to decreased Alb levels rather than to elevated globulin (Glb) levels.
Interpretation and novelty
These results suggest that EBV lytic replication was associated with hypoalbuminemia in this study.
Scientific and public-health significance
Circulating EBV-DNA status holds promise as a potential biomarker for monitoring disease progression or assessing disease severity in patients with chronic HBV infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is strong as it is based on direct measurements of viral DNA and liver function parameters.

通过机器学习实现针对慢性乙型肝炎相关肝纤维化的个性化预测

Realizing personalized prediction for the reversal of chronic hepatitis B-associated liver fibrosis through machine learning
作者： Shi-Cai Liu, Zi-Wen Wang, Han Zhang
摘要中文翻译尽管长期抗病毒药物治疗可以逆转慢性乙型肝炎患者的肝纤维化,但个体对治疗的反应差异显著,目前仍缺乏可靠的预测方法。传统的临床工具如肝硬度测量和天门冬氨酸氨基转移酶与血小板比值指数最初设计用于横断面分期,而非纵向预测,其捕捉纤维化逆转动态过程的能力有限。在本观点综述中,我们讨论了机器学习(ML)如何帮助解决这一未满足的需求。我们重点关注了机器学习在三个方面的早期但有希望的结果:利用机器学习进行图像的定量分析、通过多模态模型整合病理和临床数据,以及基于抗病毒药物治疗期间的连续测量动态预测治疗反应。我们认为,机器学习的最大价值并不在于取代现有评估工具,而在于补充它们,特别是在早期识别那些仅通过抗病毒药物治疗难以实现纤维化逆转的患者方面。然而,我们也提醒,目前大多数模型都是基于小规模、单中心的回顾性队列研究开发的,其临床应用需要前瞻性多中心验证、提高模型的可解释性以及遵循标准化的报告框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决目前缺乏可靠方法预测慢性乙型肝炎患者对治疗反应的问题,因为长期抗病毒治疗可以逆转肝纤维化,但并非所有患者都能实现逆转。
设计与对象
本观点综述讨论了机器学习在改善慢性乙型肝炎患者纤维化逆转预测方面的潜力,重点关注临床工具在捕捉动态纤维化过程方面的局限性。
核心方法
作者回顾了机器学习如何应用于分析影像数据、整合临床和病理数据,并通过在抗病毒治疗期间进行连续测量来预测患者对治疗的反应。
主要结果
机器学习在三个领域已显示出早期但有希望的结果:定量影像分析、临床和病理数据的多模态整合,以及基于连续测量的动态预测患者对治疗的反应。
解释与创新
作者解释认为,机器学习的最大价值在于补充现有工具,特别是在识别那些仅通过抗病毒药物治疗难以实现纤维化逆转的患者方面,而不是取代它们。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,机器学习可能有助于提高慢性乙型肝炎治疗的功能性治愈率,并优化个性化治疗策略,从而改善纤维化逆转的预测能力并指导临床决策。
局限与证据强度
目前的证据受到限制,因为大多数现有模型都是基于小规模、单一中心的回顾性数据,这可能影响其普适性,需要前瞻性多中心验证。

Realizing personalized prediction for the reversal of chronic hepatitis B-associated liver fibrosis through machine learning

Authors: Shi-Cai Liu, Zi-Wen Wang, Han Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Although long-term antiviral therapy can reverse liver fibrosis in a considerable proportion of patients with chronic hepatitis B, treatment response differs markedly among individuals, and reliable methods for predicting who will benefit remain lacking. Conventional clinical tools such as liver stiffness measurement and the aspartate aminotransferase-to-platelet ratio index were originally dewsigned for cross-sectional staging rather than longitudinal prediction, and their ability to capture the dynamic process of fibrosis reversal is limited. In this opinion review, we discuss how machine learning (ML) may help address this unmet need. We focus on three areas where ML has shown early but encouraging results: Quantitative analysis of images using ML, integration of pathological and clinical data through multimodal models, and dynamic prediction of treatment response based on serial measurements during antiviral therapy. In our view, the greatest value of ML lies not in replacing existing assessment tools but in complementing them, particularly through the early identification of patients who are unlikely to achieve fibrosis reversal with antiviral therapy alone. However, we also caution that most current models have been developed from small, single-center retrospective cohorts, and their clinical adoption will require prospective multi-center validation, improved model interpretability, and adherence to standardized reporting frameworks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of reliable methods for predicting individual treatment response in chronic hepatitis B patients, as long-term antiviral therapy can reverse liver fibrosis in some individuals but not all.
Design and population
This opinion review discusses the potential of machine learning to improve prediction of fibrosis reversal in chronic hepatitis B, focusing on clinical tools that are limited in capturing dynamic fibrosis processes.
Core methods
The authors review how machine learning can be applied to analyze images, integrate clinical and pathological data, and predict treatment response through serial measurements during antiviral therapy.
Main results
Machine learning has shown early but encouraging results in three areas: quantitative image analysis, multimodal integration of clinical and pathological data, and dynamic prediction of treatment response based on serial measurements.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that machine learning's greatest value lies in complementing existing tools, particularly for identifying patients unlikely to achieve fibrosis reversal with antiviral therapy alone, rather than replacing them.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that machine learning could enhance surveillance and personalized treatment strategies for chronic hepatitis B, improving the prediction of fibrosis reversal and guiding clinical decisions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the fact that most current models are developed from small, single-center retrospective cohorts, which may affect their generalizability and require prospective multi-center validation.

肝动脉灌注化疗联合仑伐替尼和拓扑替康治疗晚期乙型肝炎病毒相关肝细胞癌患者的格拉斯哥预后评分的预后价值

Prognostic value of Glasgow prognostic score in patients with advanced hepatitis-B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma undergoing hepatic arterial infusion chemotherapy combined with lenvatinib and toripalimab.
作者： Ruizhi Tang, Hongtong Tan, Han Zhu, Bingyan Jiang, Shuanggang Chen, Ying Wu, Yujia Wang, Lujun Shen, Weijun Fan
摘要中文翻译目的:本研究旨在评估格拉斯哥预后评分(GPS)在预测接受肝动脉灌注化疗(HAIC)联合仑伐替尼和拓扑替康治疗的晚期乙型肝炎病毒相关肝细胞癌(HBV-HCC)患者预后中的作用。材料与方法:本研究为单中心回顾性研究,分析了2019年3月至2021年11月期间接受治疗治疗的晚期HBV-HCC患者。根据GPS将患者分为两组,并采用倾向评分匹配(PSM)方法评估两组患者的无进展生存期(PFS)和总生存期(OS)。结果:在58名患者中,有37名被纳入研究。其中13名患者属于GPS评分0组,24名属于GPS评分1组。PSM后选取了8对患者。在PSM前,GPS评分0组患者的中位无进展生存期显著长于GPS评分1组(10.2个月 vs 4.3个月,P = 0.009);PSM后,GPS评分0组患者的中位无进展生存期仍显著长于GPS评分1组(5.6个月 vs 1.9个月,P = 0.026)。同样,GPS评分0组患者的总生存期在PSM前(32.0个月 vs 9.7个月,P < 0.001)和PSM后(32.0个月 vs 8.6个月,P < 0.001)均显著更长。GPS被确定为总生存期(OS)的独立预后因素,对无进展生存期(PFS)具有边缘显著性。此外,肿瘤数量被确定为无进展生存期(PFS)的独立预后因素。结论:这项探索性研究提供了初步证据,表明GPS可能作为接受三联疗法的晚期乙型肝炎病毒相关肝细胞癌(HBV-HCC)患者的预后因素,需要在更大规模的队列研究研究中进一步验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估格拉斯哥预后评分(GPS)在预测接受肝动脉灌注化疗(HAIC)联合仑伐替尼和拓扑替康治疗的晚期乙型肝炎病毒相关肝细胞癌(HBV-HCC)患者预后中的作用。
设计与对象

核心方法
本研究采用倾向评分匹配(PSM)方法,对根据格拉斯哥预后评分(GPS)分组的患者进行无进展生存期(PFS)和总生存期(OS)的评估。
主要结果
在58名患者中,有37名被纳入研究。其中13名患者属于GPS评分0组,24名属于GPS评分1组。PSM后选取了8对患者。
解释与创新
在PSM前,GPS评分0组患者的中位无进展生存期显著长于GPS评分1组(10.2个月 vs 4.3个月,P = 0.009);PSM后,GPS评分0组患者的中位无进展生存期仍显著长于GPS评分1组(5.6个月 vs 1.9个月,P = 0.026)。同样,GPS评分0组患者的总生存期在PSM前(32.0个月 vs 9.7个月,P < 0.001)和PSM后(32.0个月 vs 8.6个月,P < 0.001)均显著更长。
科研与公卫意义
GPS被确定为总生存期(OS)的独立预后因素,对无进展生存期(PFS)具有边缘显著性。此外,肿瘤数量被确定为无进展生存期(PFS)的独立预后因素。

这项探索性研究提供了初步证据,表明GPS可能作为接受三联疗法的晚期乙型肝炎病毒相关肝细胞癌(HBV-HCC)患者的预后因素,需要在更大规模的队列研究研究中进一步验证。

Prognostic value of Glasgow prognostic score in patients with advanced hepatitis-B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma undergoing hepatic arterial infusion chemotherapy combined with lenvatinib and toripalimab.

Authors: Ruizhi Tang, Hongtong Tan, Han Zhu, Bingyan Jiang, Shuanggang Chen, Ying Wu, Yujia Wang, Lujun Shen, Weijun Fan
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: This study aimed to evaluate the Glasgow Prognostic Score (GPS) in predicting prognosis in patients with advanced hepatitis-B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HBV-HCC) who are undergoing hepatic arterial infusion chemotherapy (HAIC) combined with lenvatinib and toripalimab therapy. MATERIALS AND METHODS: In this single-center retrospective study, patients with advanced HBV-HCC who received treatment with HAIC combined with lenvatinib and toripalimab from March 2019 to November 2021 were analyzed. The patients were divided into two groups based on GPS, and propensity score matching (PSM) was used to assess progression-free survival (PFS) and overall survival (OS) in both groups. RESULTS: Of the 58 patients, 37 were included in the study. Thirteen patients were in the GPS score 0 group and 24 in the GPS score 1 group. After PSM, eight pairs were selected. Patients in the GPS score 0 group had significantly longer median PFS before (10.2 versus 4.3 months, P = 0.009) and after PSM (5.6 versus 1.9 months, P = 0.026). Similarly, OS was significantly longer in the GPS score 0 group before (32.0 versus 9.7 months, P < 0.001) and after PSM (32.0 versus 8.6 months, P < 0.001). GPS was an independent prognostic factor for OS, with borderline significance for PFS. In addition, tumor number was identified as an independent prognostic factor for PFS. CONCLUSION: This exploratory study provides preliminary evidence that GPS may serve as a prognostic factor for patients with advanced HBV-related HCC who were receiving a triple-combination therapy, requiring further validation in larger cohorts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the Glasgow Prognostic Score (GPS) in predicting prognosis in patients with advanced hepatitis-B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HBV-HCC) who are undergoing hepatic arterial infusion chemotherapy (HAIC) combined with lenvatinib and toripalimab therapy.

The patients were divided into two groups based on GPS, and propensity score matching (PSM) was used to assess progression-free survival (PFS) and overall survival (OS) in both groups.
Core methods
The study used propensity score matching (PSM) to assess progression-free survival (PFS) and overall survival (OS) in patients divided into two groups based on their Glasgow Prognostic Score (GPS).
Main results
Of the 58 patients, 37 were included in the study. Thirteen patients were in the GPS score 0 group and 24 in the GPS score 1 group. After PSM, eight pairs were selected.

Similarly, OS was significantly longer in the GPS score 0 group before (32.0 versus 9.7 months, P < 0.001) and after PSM (32.0 versus 8.6 months, P < 0.001).

Limitations and evidence strength
This exploratory study provides preliminary evidence that GPS may serve as a prognostic factor for patients with advanced HBV-related HCC who were receiving a triple-combination therapy, requiring further validation in larger cohorts.

系统性炎症与疫苗诱导的乙型肝炎表面抗原(anti-HBs)水平持续性在医疗工作者中的关系

Systemic Inflammation and the Persistence of Vaccine-Induced Hepatitis B Immunity in Healthcare Workers
作者： Bestegül Çoruh Akyol, Tuğba Dağaşan, Soner Çankaya, Özgür Enginyurt, Enginyurt Ö
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨系统性炎症与疫苗诱导的乙型肝炎表面抗原(anti-HBs)水平持续性在医疗工作者中的关系,因为乙型肝炎表面抗原(anti-HBs)水平的长期持续性因人而异,系统性炎症的作用尚不明确。
设计与对象
本研究是一项具有回顾性成分的纵向观察性研究,纳入了2023年至2025年间接受常规职业健康筛查的583名医疗工作者。研究评估了anti-HBs抗体水平与系统性炎症标志物之间的关系。
核心方法
Anti-HBs levels at 2 time points were analyzed together with C-reactive protein (CRP) level, neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio, platelet-to-lymphocyte ratio, systemic immune-inflammation index, and systemic inflammation response index. Nonparametric and correlation analyses were performed.
主要结果
Anti-HBs levels decreased significantly between baseline and follow-up (Hodges-Lehmann estimate: -23.00 mIU/mL, 95% CI: -27.60 to -18.93; P<0.001). No significant associations were observed between antibody levels and sex, smoking status, or the neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio, platelet-to-lymphocyte ratio, systemic immune-inflammation index, and systemic inflammation response index. CRP positivity was associated with lower anti-HBs levels at the second measurement, but no association was observed between CRP and longitudinal change in anti-HBs levels.
解释与创新
作者认为,CRP在第二次测量时与较低的anti-HBs水平存在弱的横断面关联,但未观察到CRP或其他炎症指标与anti-HBs水平纵向变化之间的关联。这些发现表明,系统性炎症可能不是anti-HBs水平纵向持续性的决定因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,系统性炎症标志物可能对监测医疗工作者的疫苗接种免疫无实际价值,需要进一步的纵向研究来确定其临床应用价值。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其观察性设计以及未能控制潜在的混杂因素。证据强度中等,因为研究结果并未建立系统性炎症与抗体持续性之间的因果关系。

Systemic Inflammation and the Persistence of Vaccine-Induced Hepatitis B Immunity in Healthcare Workers

Authors: Bestegül Çoruh Akyol, Tuğba Dağaşan, Soner Çankaya, Özgür Enginyurt, Enginyurt Ö
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND The long-term persistence of hepatitis B surface antigen (anti-HBs) levels varies among individuals, and the role of systemic inflammation remains unclear. This study evaluated the relationship between anti-HBs antibody levels and systemic inflammatory markers in healthcare workers. MATERIAL AND METHODS This longitudinal observational study with a retrospective component included 583 healthcare workers who underwent routine occupational health screening between 2023 and 2025. Anti-HBs levels at 2 time points were analyzed together with C-reactive protein (CRP) level, neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio, platelet-to-lymphocyte ratio, systemic immune-inflammation index, and systemic inflammation response index. Nonparametric and correlation analyses were performed. RESULTS Anti-HBs levels decreased significantly between baseline and follow-up (Hodges-Lehmann estimate: -23.00 mIU/mL, 95% CI: -27.60 to -18.93; P<0.001). No significant associations were observed between antibody levels and sex, smoking status, or the neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio, platelet-to-lymphocyte ratio, systemic immune-inflammation index, and systemic inflammation response index. In contrast, CRP positivity was associated with lower anti-HBs levels at the second measurement; however, no association was observed between CRP and longitudinal change in anti-HBs levels. Higher anti-HBs levels were observed among physicians and healthcare workers in the very high occupational biological-risk group, whereas age showed a weak negative correlation with antibody levels. CONCLUSIONS CRP showed a weak cross-sectional association with lower anti-HBs levels at the second measurement, whereas no association was observed between CRP or the other inflammatory indices and longitudinal change in anti-HBs levels. Therefore, the present findings do not establish systemic inflammation as a determinant of longitudinal antibody persistence. Further longitudinal studies are required to determine whether systemic inflammatory markers have clinical utility in post-vaccination immunity monitoring.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the relationship between systemic inflammation and the persistence of vaccine-induced hepatitis B immunity in healthcare workers, as the long-term persistence of hepatitis B surface antigen (anti-HBs) levels varies among individuals, and the role of systemic inflammation remains unclear.
Design and population
This longitudinal observational study with a retrospective component included 583 healthcare workers who underwent routine occupational health screening between 2023 and 2025. The study evaluated the relationship between anti-HBs antibody levels and systemic inflammatory markers.
Core methods
Anti-HBs levels at 2 time points were analyzed together with C-reactive protein (CRP) level, neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio, platelet-to-lymphocyte ratio, systemic immune-inflammation index, and systemic inflammation response index. Nonparametric and correlation analyses were performed.
Main results
Anti-HBs levels decreased significantly between baseline and follow-up (Hodges-Lehmann estimate: -23.00 mIU/mL, 95% CI: -27.60 to -18.93; P<0.001). No significant associations were observed between antibody levels and sex, smoking status, or the neutrophil-to-lymphocyte ratio, platelet-to-lymphocyte ratio, systemic immune-inflammation index, and systemic inflammation response index. CRP positivity was associated with lower anti-HBs levels at the second measurement, but no association was observed between CRP and longitudinal change in anti-HBs levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that CRP showed a weak cross-sectional association with lower anti-HBs levels at the second measurement, but no association was observed between CRP or the other inflammatory indices and longitudinal change in anti-HBs levels. These findings suggest that systemic inflammation may not be a determinant of longitudinal antibody persistence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings indicate that systemic inflammatory markers may not be useful for monitoring post-vaccination immunity in healthcare workers, and further longitudinal studies are needed to determine their clinical utility.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and the lack of control for potential confounding factors. The evidence is moderate, as the results do not establish a causal relationship between systemic inflammation and antibody persistence.

家庭聚集筛查可能有助于识别来自高流行地区的乙型肝炎病毒携带者感染乙型肝炎病毒的情况。

Family cluster screening may help identify hepatitis D virus infections among individuals from highly endemic regions.
作者： Carlos Alventosa-Mateu, María Dolores Ocete Mochón, Patricia Escrivá Pastor, Juan José Urquijo Ponce, C. Alventosa Mateu
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒(HDV)感染是最严重的慢性病毒性肝炎形式,可能在来自高流行地区的个体中显著未被诊断。我们报告了一例西班牙居住的索引病例通过筛查后发现的罗马尼亚家庭聚集情况。六名家庭成员和一级亲属接受了乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和HDV筛查,发现了三例额外的慢性HDV感染病例。值得注意的是,所有受影响的个体在筛查时均患有晚期肝病,其中两人随后需要进行肝移植。两名患者报告称他们曾知晓自己的HBV感染状况,但已不再接受医疗随访且未进行HDV筛查。该家庭聚集情况突显了对家庭成员和一级亲属进行级联筛查在识别HDV感染方面的潜在价值,特别是在来自HDV流行地区的家庭中。它还说明了延迟筛查的临床后果,包括出现晚期肝病和治疗机会有限。因此,家庭聚集病例发现可能代表一种在高风险人群中系统性HDV筛查的有用补充策略。

Family cluster screening may help identify hepatitis D virus infections among individuals from highly endemic regions.

Authors: Carlos Alventosa-Mateu, María Dolores Ocete Mochón, Patricia Escrivá Pastor, Juan José Urquijo Ponce, C. Alventosa Mateu
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis D virus (HDV) infection is the most severe form of chronic viral hepatitis and may remain substantially underdiagnosed, particularly among individuals originating from highly endemic regions. We report a Romanian family cluster identified through cascade screening following the diagnosis of an index case living in Spain. Six household contacts and first-degree relatives underwent hepatitis B virus (HBV) and HDV screening, identifying three additional cases of chronic HDV infection. Notably, all affected individuals had advanced liver disease at diagnosis, and two subsequently required liver transplantation. Two patients reported having been aware of their HBV diagnosis but were no longer engaged to healthcare follow-up and had not undergone HDV screening. This family cluster highlights the potential value of cascade screening of household contacts and first-degree relatives for identifying HDV infection, particularly among families originating from HDV-endemic regions. It also illustrates the clinical consequences of delayed diagnosis, including presentation with advanced liver disease and limited therapeutic opportunities. Family cluster case-finding could therefore represent a useful complementary strategy to systematic HDV screening in at-risk populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis D virus (HDV) infection is the most severe form of chronic viral hepatitis and may remain substantially underdiagnosed, particularly among individuals originating from highly endemic regions.
Design and population
We report a Romanian family cluster identified through cascade screening following the diagnosis of an index case living in Spain.
Core methods
Six household contacts and first-degree relatives underwent hepatitis B virus (HBV) and HDV screening, identifying three additional cases of chronic HDV infection.
Main results
Two patients reported having been aware of their HBV diagnosis but were no longer engaged to healthcare follow-up and had not undergone HDV screening.
Interpretation and novelty
This family cluster highlights the potential value of cascade screening of household contacts and first-degree relatives for identifying HDV infection, particularly among families originating from HDV-endemic regions.
Scientific and public-health significance
It also illustrates the clinical consequences of delayed diagnosis, including presentation with advanced liver disease and limited therapeutic opportunities.
Limitations and evidence strength
Family cluster case-finding could therefore represent a useful complementary strategy to systematic HDV screening in at-risk populations.

关于喀麦隆半农村地区Obala卫生区健康专业人员对预防乙型肝炎病毒母婴传播传播知识、态度和实践的调查:一项横断面研究

Knowledge, attitudes and practices regarding prevention of hepatitis B virus mother-to-child transmission among health professionals in a semi-rural health district of Cameroon: a cross-sectional study
作者： Guy-Roger Ebanda Enamba, Manelle Ida Toukem Teguim, Tatiana Mossus, Rose Emmanuelle Abomo Enamba, Mbinyui Nyiniwou Sidiki, Edmond François Tchago, Gérard Nkeunen, Marie-José Essi
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染仍然是喀麦隆的主要公共卫生问题,该国为HBV高流行地区,母婴传播传播占慢性感染的相当大比例,其中超过90%的出生时获得的感染会发展为慢性感染,而成人中则少于5%。预防HBV母婴传播传播(PMTCT)直接取决于提供产科和免疫接种服务的卫生工作者的知识、态度和实践,这些在喀麦隆的半城市和农村卫生区中仍缺乏充分记录。本研究旨在评估Obala卫生区健康专业人员在这三个维度上的情况,并确定与每个维度相关的因素。方法 2026年4月至6月期间,在喀麦隆Centre地区Obala卫生区的33个卫生机构中,对提供产前检查、产科和免疫接种服务的卫生工作者进行了分析性横断面研究。所有145名符合条件的工作人员均被充分接触;数据通过结构化的、预测试的问卷在严格保密和匿名条件下收集,并使用IBM SPSS版本27.0进行分析。得分以理论最大值的百分比表示,并在85%的阈值处进行二分类(良好/适当/充分与其余类别合并)以进行多变量分析。多变量二元逻辑回归分析确定了与良好知识、适当态度和充分实践相关的因素,p
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染仍然是喀麦隆的主要公共卫生问题,该国为HBV高流行地区,母婴传播传播占慢性感染的相当大比例,其中超过90%的出生时获得的感染会发展为慢性感染,而成人中则少于5%。
设计与对象
本研究旨在评估Obala卫生区健康专业人员在这三个维度上的情况,并确定与每个维度相关的因素。
核心方法
2026年4月至6月期间,在喀麦隆Centre地区Obala卫生区的33个卫生机构中,对提供产前检查、产科和免疫接种服务的卫生工作者进行了分析性横断面研究。所有145名符合条件的工作人员均被充分接触;数据通过结构化的、预测试的问卷在严格保密和匿名条件下收集,并使用IBM SPSS版本27.0进行分析。
主要结果
得分以理论最大值的百分比表示,并在85%的阈值处进行二分类(良好/适当/充分与其余类别合并)以进行多变量分析。
解释与创新
预防HBV母婴传播传播(PMTCT)直接取决于提供产科和免疫接种服务的卫生工作者的知识、态度和实践,这些在喀麦隆的半城市和农村卫生区中仍缺乏充分记录。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为改善喀麦隆半农村地区卫生专业人员对PMTCT的知识、态度和实践提供有针对性的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于摘要分析,由于缺乏详细结果和统计分析,证据强度中等。

Knowledge, attitudes and practices regarding prevention of hepatitis B virus mother-to-child transmission among health professionals in a semi-rural health district of Cameroon: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Guy-Roger Ebanda Enamba, Manelle Ida Toukem Teguim, Tatiana Mossus, Rose Emmanuelle Abomo Enamba, Mbinyui Nyiniwou Sidiki, Edmond François Tchago, Gérard Nkeunen, Marie-José Essi
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health problem in Cameroon, a country of high endemicity where mother-to-child transmission accounts for a substantial proportion of chronic infections, with more than 90% of infections acquired at birth progressing to chronicity compared with less than 5% among adults. Prevention of HBV mother-to-child transmission (PMTCT) depends directly on the knowledge, attitudes, and practices of health workers providing maternity and immunization services, which remain poorly documented in semi-urban and rural health districts of Cameroon. This study aimed to assess these three dimensions among health professionals in the Obala Health District and to identify the factors associated with each. Methods An analytical cross-sectional study was conducted from April to June 2026 among health workers providing antenatal, maternity, and immunization services across the 33 health facilities of the Obala Health District, Centre Region, Cameroon. All 145 eligible staff were approached exhaustively; data were collected using a structured, pre-tested questionnaire under strict conditions of anonymity and confidentiality, and analyzed using IBM SPSS version 27.0. Scores were expressed as a percentage of the theoretical maximum and dichotomized at the 85% threshold (good/appropriate/adequate versus the remaining categories combined) for multivariable analysis. Multivariable binary logistic regression identified factors associated with good knowledge, appropriate attitudes, and adequate practices at p
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health problem in Cameroon, a country of high endemicity where mother-to-child transmission accounts for a substantial proportion of chronic infections, with more than 90% of infections acquired at birth progressing to chronicity compared with less than 5% among adults.
Design and population
This study aimed to assess these three dimensions among health professionals in the Obala Health District and to identify the factors associated with each.
Core methods
An analytical cross-sectional study was conducted from April to June 2026 among health workers providing antenatal, maternity, and immunization services across the 33 health facilities of the Obala Health District, Centre Region, Cameroon. All 145 eligible staff were approached exhaustively; data were collected using a structured, pre-tested questionnaire under strict conditions of anonymity and confidentiality, and analyzed using IBM SPSS version 27.0.
Main results
Scores were expressed as a percentage of the theoretical maximum and dichotomized at the 85% threshold (good/appropriate/adequate versus the remaining categories combined) for multivariable analysis.
Interpretation and novelty
Prevention of HBV mother-to-child transmission (PMTCT) depends directly on the knowledge, attitudes, and practices of health workers providing maternity and immunization services, which remain poorly documented in semi-urban and rural health districts of Cameroon.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted interventions to improve health professionals' knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding PMTCT in semi-rural health districts of Cameroon.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed results and statistical analyses.

终末期肾病患者接受维持性血液透析时对乙型肝炎病毒疫苗接种的抗体反应预测因素。

Predictors of Antibody Response to Hepatitis B Vaccination in Hemodialysis Patients.
作者： R. D. Gupta, P K Dutta, M. A. Islam, R. K. Saha, S. Dutta, S R Khan
摘要中文翻译接受维持性血液透析的终末期肾病(ESRD)患者感染乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)的风险较高,并且对疫苗接种的抗体反应不足。本研究的目的是评估接受乙型肝炎疫苗接种的维持性血液透析患者的抗体反应(anti-HBs水平)。这项横断面观察性分析研究在孟加拉国吉大港的海洋城医学院医院进行,持续时间为34个月,研究对象为接受维持性血液透析超过4个月的50名患者。收集了人口统计学和临床数据,并测量了接种疫苗接种后的anti-HBs滴度。患者被分为高反应者(anti-HBs ≥100 mIU/ml)和低反应者(anti-HBs ≥10至50岁,糖尿病的存在以及较低的血清白蛋白水平与不良或无反应显著相关(p<0.05)。维持性血液透析患者接种乙型肝炎疫苗接种后产生保护性抗体水平不足。年龄较大、糖尿病和低血清白蛋白是不良反应的重要预测因素。通过加强剂量接种和重新接种等策略,可能提高该人群的血清转换率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
血液透析患者感染乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)的风险较高,并且对疫苗接种的抗体反应不足。本研究旨在评估这些患者群体中对乙型肝炎疫苗接种的抗体反应预测因素。
设计与对象
这项横断面观察性分析研究在孟加拉国吉大港的海洋城医学院医院进行,持续时间为34个月,研究对象为接受维持性血液透析超过4个月的50名患者。
核心方法
收集了人口统计学和临床数据,并测量了接种疫苗接种后的anti-HBs滴度,以将患者分为高反应者或低反应者。
主要结果
年龄较大、糖尿病的存在以及较低的血清白蛋白水平与对乙型肝炎疫苗接种的不良或无反应显著相关(p<0.05)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,某些临床因素是维持性血液透析患者对乙型肝炎疫苗接种抗体反应不足的重要预测因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为通过针对性干预措施(如加强剂量接种和重新接种)提高维持性血液透析患者的血清转换率提供依据。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到观察性设计和小样本量的限制,但鉴于临床因素与不良反应之间的明确关联,其证据是强有力的。

Predictors of Antibody Response to Hepatitis B Vaccination in Hemodialysis Patients.

Authors: R. D. Gupta, P K Dutta, M. A. Islam, R. K. Saha, S. Dutta, S R Khan
Original abstract (English)
End-stage renal disease (ESRD) patients receiving maintenance hemodialysis are at high risk of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection and demonstrate inadequate immune response to vaccination. The objective of the study was to assess the antibody response (anti-HBs levels) of hepatitis B vaccinated maintenance hemodialysis patients. This cross-sectional observational analytical study was conducted at Marine City Medical College Hospital, Chittagong, Bangladesh for 34 months of period among 50 patients receiving maintenance hemodialysis for more than 4 months. Demographic and clinical data were collected and post-vaccination anti-HBs titers were measured. Patients were classified as high responders (anti-HBs ≥100 mIU/ml), poor responders (anti-HBs ≥10 to 50 years, presence of diabetes mellitus and lower serum albumin were significantly associated with poor or non response (p<0.05). Development of protective antibody levels after hepatitis B vaccination in maintenance hemodialysis patients was inadequate. Older age, diabetes and low serum albumin were important predictors of poor response. Strategies such as booster dosing and revaccination may improve seroconversion rates in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hemodialysis patients are at high risk of hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection and show inadequate immune response to vaccination. The study aimed to assess predictors of antibody response to hepatitis B vaccination in this population.
Design and population
A cross-sectional observational analytical study was conducted at Marine City Medical College Hospital, Chittagong, Bangladesh over 34 months among 50 patients receiving maintenance hemodialysis for more than 4 months.
Core methods
Demographic and clinical data were collected, and post-vaccination anti-HBs titers were measured to classify patients as high responders or poor responders.
Main results
Older age, presence of diabetes mellitus, and lower serum albumin were significantly associated with poor or non-response to hepatitis B vaccination (p<0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight that certain clinical factors are important predictors of inadequate antibody response to hepatitis B vaccination in hemodialysis patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform strategies for improving seroconversion rates in hemodialysis patients through targeted interventions such as booster dosing and revaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and small sample size, but the evidence is strong given the clear association between clinical factors and poor response.

开伯尔-普什图省乙型肝炎病毒和丙型肝炎病毒患者临床、人口统计学和心血管代谢特征的比较:一项横断面研究

Comparative Clinical, Demographic, and Cardiometabolic Profile of Hepatitis B and Hepatitis C Patients in Khyber Pakhtunkhwa: A Cross- Sectional Study
作者： Muhammad Tariq Shah, Tahir Ullah, Hamza Nasir, Abdul Qayum Khan, Maxwell Afram
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景与目的 巴基斯坦的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)感染情况异常严重,仍然是慢性肝病的重要病因。尽管在开伯尔-普什图省(Khyber Pakhtunkhwa)缺乏相关证据,但全球数据显示,感染个体在心血管代谢和人口统计学特征上存在差异。本研究通过比较HBV和HCV感染个体的人口统计学、心血管代谢和临床特征,以确定肝炎类型的独立预测因素。方法 这项比较性横断面研究纳入了549名实验室确诊的患者(249名HBV阳性,300名HCV阳性),来自巴基斯坦开伯尔-普什图省。人口统计学、人体测量学、心血管代谢、疫苗接种以及症状数据均从临床记录中提取;高血压采用2017年ACC/AHA标准定义,临床症状被分为七个临床领域。通过Shapiro-Wilk检验评估数据的正态性;对于非正态分布的连续变量,采用Mann-Whitney U检验进行组间比较,而分类变量则采用Pearson卡方检验,并以Cramér's V作为效应量。通过多变量二元逻辑回归分析确定与肝炎类型独立相关的因素,模型拟合度通过Hosmer-Lemeshow拟合优度检验和Nagelkerke R2进行评估。结果 HBV患者的年龄显著高于HCV患者(41.03±10.61岁 vs. 36.64±10.35岁,p < 0.001)。高血压是肝炎类型最强的独立相关因素。结论 尽管HBV和HCV患者的症状表现相似,但两者在心血管代谢风险特征上存在显著差异,这表明在管理这些感染时需要采取针对性的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在比较巴基斯坦开伯尔-普什图省HBV和HCV感染个体的人口统计学、临床和心血管代谢特征,以确定肝炎类型的独立预测因素。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究纳入了549名实验室确诊的患者(249名HBV阳性和300名HCV阳性),这些患者于2022年至2024年间在巴基斯坦开伯尔-普什图省招募。
核心方法
人口统计学、人体测量学、心血管代谢、疫苗接种以及症状数据均从临床记录中提取。高血压采用2017年ACC/AHA标准定义,临床症状被分为七个临床领域。统计分析包括使用Shapiro-Wilk检验评估正态性,使用Mann-Whitney U检验分析非正态分布的连续变量,使用Pearson卡方检验并以Cramér's V作为效应量分析分类变量。采用多变量二元逻辑回归分析确定肝炎类型的独立预测因素,模型拟合度通过Hosmer-Lemeshow拟合优度检验和Nagelkerke R2进行评估。
主要结果
HBV患者的年龄显著高于HCV患者(41.03±10.61岁 vs. 36.64±10.35岁,p < 0.001)。高血压是肝炎类型最强的独立相关因素。
解释与创新
研究结果突显了HBV和HCV患者之间在心血管代谢风险特征上的显著差异,尽管两者症状表现相似,这表明在管理这些感染时需要采取针对性的策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提供了HBV和HCV感染在人口统计学和心血管代谢方面的差异信息,这可以为开伯尔-普什图省的针对性监测和管理策略提供依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于巴基斯坦一个地区的数据,且基于临床记录,这可能引入偏倚。由于这些限制,研究结果的可信度中等。

Comparative Clinical, Demographic, and Cardiometabolic Profile of Hepatitis B and Hepatitis C Patients in Khyber Pakhtunkhwa: A Cross- Sectional Study

Authors: Muhammad Tariq Shah, Tahir Ullah, Hamza Nasir, Abdul Qayum Khan, Maxwell Afram
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background and Objectives Pakistan is disproportionately affected by HBV and HCV, which continue to be important causes of chronic liver disease. Although there is little evidence in Khyber Pakhtunkhwa, worldwide data indicates that infected individuals have different cardiometabolic and demographic characteristics. This study identified independent predictors of hepatitis type by comparing the demographic, cardiometabolic, and clinical characteristics of individuals with HBV and HCV. Methodology This comparative cross-sectional study included 549 laboratory-confirmed patients (249 HBV-positive, 300 HCV-positive) recruited from Khyber Pakhtunkhwa, Pakistan. Demographic, anthropometric, cardiometabolic, vaccination, and symptom data were extracted from clinical records; hypertension was defined using the 2017 ACC/AHA threshold, and presenting symptoms were grouped into seven clinical domains. Normality was assessed using the Shapiro-Wilk test; as continuous variables were non-normally distributed, between-group comparisons used the Mann-Whitney U test, while categorical variables were compared using the Pearson Chi-square test with Cramér's V as effect size. Multivariable binary logistic regression was performed to identify factors independently associated with hepatitis type, with model fit assessed via the Hosmer-Lemeshow goodness-of-fit test and Nagelkerke R2. Results HBV patients were significantly older than HCV patients (41.03±10.61 vs. 36.64±10.35 years, p Conclusion HBV and HCV patients exhibit distinct cardiometabolic risk profiles despite comparable symptom presentation. Hypertension emerged as the strongest independent correlate of hepatitis type, underscoring the value of adjusted analysis in hepatitis comorbidity research.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare the demographic, clinical, and cardiometabolic profiles of individuals with hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) infections in Khyber Pakhtunkhwa, Pakistan, to identify independent predictors of hepatitis type.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study included 549 laboratory-confirmed patients (249 HBV-positive and 300 HCV-positive) recruited from Khyber Pakhtunkhwa, Pakistan, between 2022 and 2024.
Core methods
Demographic, anthropometric, cardiometabolic, vaccination, and symptom data were extracted from clinical records. Hypertension was defined using the 2017 ACC/AHA threshold, and presenting symptoms were grouped into seven clinical domains. Statistical analyses included the Shapiro-Wilk test for normality, Mann-Whitney U test for non-normally distributed continuous variables, and Pearson Chi-square test with Cramér's V as effect size for categorical variables. Multivariable binary logistic regression was used to identify independent predictors of hepatitis type, with model fit assessed via the Hosmer-Lemeshow goodness-of-fit test and Nagelkerke R2.
Main results
HBV patients were significantly older than HCV patients (41.03±10.61 vs. 36.64±10.35 years, p < 0.001). Hypertension emerged as the strongest independent correlate of hepatitis type.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight distinct cardiometabolic risk profiles between HBV and HCV patients, despite comparable symptom presentation, suggesting the need for tailored approaches in managing these infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the demographic and cardiometabolic differences between HBV and HCV infections, which can inform targeted surveillance and management strategies in Khyber Pakhtunkhwa.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to data from a single region in Pakistan, and the evidence is based on clinical records, which may introduce bias. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to these limitations.

绘制拉丁美洲和加勒比地区的 乙型肝炎病毒 研究和基因组多样性

Mapping Hepatitis B Virus Research and Genomic Diversity in Latin America and the Caribbean
作者： Alexis Jose-Abrego, Sonia Roman, Irene M. Mariscal-Martinez, Karina Gonzalez-Aldaco, Arturo Panduro, Alexis José-Ábrego, Sonia Román, Karina González-Aldaco
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染仍然是拉丁美洲和加勒比地区(LAC)的主要公共卫生挑战。绘制科学生产图谱和病毒基因组多样性对于识别已确立的研究领域以及改善和扩展该地区研究的机会至关重要。方法:对PubMed、Scopus和Web of Science(1970–2025)中索引的HBV文献以及9528条GenBank序列(包括完整的乙型肝炎病毒基因组、单个基因和部分诊断片段)进行了整合分析,结合了文献计量学、文本挖掘和系统发育分析。结果:共识别出4083篇文献,其中72.1%(2942/4083)来自巴西、墨西哥和阿根廷。大多数为原始研究文章(75.8%;2749/3628),涉及人类乙型肝炎病毒(95.8%;2471/2580),主要研究领域集中在血清学特征分析、共感染、慢性乙型肝炎、基因型特征分析和血液安全方面,而肝细胞癌、慢性乙型肝炎治疗和代谢共病则是研究较少的领域。体外研究(10.1%;260/2580)和动物模型(6.9%;177/2580)仅占文献的一小部分。检测到8种流行的HBV基因型(A–H),其中A型(43.4%;4138/9528)、F型(29.3%;2787/9528)和D型(19.8%;1885/9528)占主导地位,而H型基因型主要局限于墨西哥(3.2%;306/9528)。观察到显著的地理聚集性(p < 0.001),反映了本地的美洲和移民的病毒谱系。结论:流行病学研究是LAC中最稳固的研究领域,但研究仍集中在少数国家,存在持续的实验和临床研究空白。促进跨境合作并投资于未被充分探索的转化研究领域,将是实现该地区乙肝消除的关键。

Mapping Hepatitis B Virus Research and Genomic Diversity in Latin America and the Caribbean

Authors: Alexis Jose-Abrego, Sonia Roman, Irene M. Mariscal-Martinez, Karina Gonzalez-Aldaco, Arturo Panduro, Alexis José-Ábrego, Sonia Román, Karina González-Aldaco
Original abstract (English)
Background: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health challenge in Latin America and the Caribbean (LAC). Mapping scientific production and viral genomic diversity is essential to identify consolidated research fields, as well as areas of opportunity to improve and expand research across the region. Methods: An integrated analysis combining bibliometrics, text-mining, and phylogenetics was performed on HBV literature indexed in PubMed, Scopus, and Web of Science (1970–2025), alongside 9528 GenBank sequences (including full-length genomes, individual genes, and partial diagnostic fragments). Results: A total of 4083 publications were identified, of which 72.1% (2942/4083) were from Brazil, Mexico, and Argentina. Most were original articles (75.8%; 2749/3628) involving human cohorts (95.8%; 2471/2580), where primary research fields centered on serological profiling, co-infections, vaccination, genotype characterization, and blood safety, whereas hepatocellular carcinoma, antiviral therapy, and metabolic comorbidities were the least frequently studied areas. In vitro (10.1%; 260/2580) and animal models (6.9%; 177/2580) accounted for a minor fraction of the literature. Eight circulating HBV genotypes (A–H) were detected, dominated by A (43.4%; 4138/9528), F (29.3%; 2787/9528), and D (19.8%; 1885/9528), while genotype H was largely confined to Mexico (3.2%; 306/9528). Marked geographic clustering was observed (p < 0.001), reflecting both autochthonous American and immigrant lineages. Conclusions: Epidemiological studies represent the most consolidated domain in LAC, yet research remains concentrated in a few countries with persistent experimental and clinical gaps. Fostering cross-border collaboration and investing in underexplored translational areas will be decisive in achieving regional hepatitis B elimination.

Question and background
Mapping scientific production and viral genomic diversity is essential to identify consolidated research fields, as well as areas of opportunity to improve and expand research across the region.
