基加利卢旺达男同性恋者和跨性别女性中乙型肝炎病毒和丙型肝炎病毒的患病率及其相关因素

The prevalence of and factors associated with hepatitis B and hepatitis C among men who have sex with men in Kigali, Rwanda
作者： Jean Olivier Twahirwa Rwema, Eric Niyongira, Nneoma E. Okonkwo, Jean Bizimana, Jean Damascene Makuza, Matthew M. Hamill, Carrie E. Lyons, Julien Nyombayire, Gallican Nshogoza Rwibasira, Patrick Sullivan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述基加利卢旺达男同性恋者(MSM)和跨性别女性(TGW)中乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)的流行病学,以指导预防和治疗计划。
设计与对象
在卢旺达基加利进行了一项横断面研究,使用受访者驱动抽样(RDS)方法,于2018年3月至8月期间招募了729名MSM/TGW参与者。平均年龄为25岁(IQR:22–31;范围:18–68),其中14.4%(n = 106)为TGW。
核心方法
使用快速诊断测试对储存的血浆样本进行HBV表面抗原(HBsAg)和HCV抗体(HCVAb)检测。对所有阳性样本进行了HBV和HCV的病毒载量(VL)检测。采用近似贝叶斯逻辑回归模型,通过数据增强进行惩罚似然估计,以评估与HBsAg和HCVAb阳性相关的因素。
主要结果
HBsAg的患病率为3.4%(25/729;RDS调整后:2.7%,95%CI:1.2% – 4.2%)。其中,92.0%(23/25)检测到可检测的HBV DNA,中位数为3.0(IQR:1.9–3.7)log10 IU/ml。HCVAb在1.6%(12/729;RDS调整后:1.7%,95%CI:0.7% − 2.7%)的样本中被检测到,其中包括33.3%(4/12)检测到可检测的HCV RNA。
解释与创新
年龄、有性伴侣曾注射毒品以及在研究入组前一年报告性传播疾病(STI)症状与HBsAg阳性相关。年龄是唯一与HCVAb阳性显著相关的因素,突显了针对年长MSM/TGW制定针对性干预措施的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了在卢旺达持续对MSM和TGW进行病毒性肝炎筛查的重要性,特别是针对35岁及以上的个体,以加强病毒性肝炎预防、诊断、治疗和肝细胞癌(HCC)筛查计划。
局限与证据强度
本研究使用了储存样本,并依赖于自我报告数据,这可能引入回忆偏差。由于研究设计为横断面,且可能无法准确捕捉动态风险行为,因此证据强度中等。

The prevalence of and factors associated with hepatitis B and hepatitis C among men who have sex with men in Kigali, Rwanda

Authors: Jean Olivier Twahirwa Rwema, Eric Niyongira, Nneoma E. Okonkwo, Jean Bizimana, Jean Damascene Makuza, Matthew M. Hamill, Carrie E. Lyons, Julien Nyombayire, Gallican Nshogoza Rwibasira, Patrick Sullivan
Original abstract (English)
Rwanda has scaled up its viral hepatitis B (HBV) and C (HCV) prevention and treatment programs over the last decade. However, data to guide HBV and HCV programming for men who have sex with men (MSM) and transgender women (TGW) are limited. This analysis characterizes the epidemiology of HBV and HCV among MSM and TGW in Kigali, Rwanda. We leveraged stored plasma samples of 729 MSM/TGW recruited in Kigali using respondent-driven sampling (RDS) conducted between March and August 2018. Rapid diagnostic tests were used to screen for HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) and HCV antibodies (HCVAb). Viral load (VL) testing for HBV and HCV was performed for all positive samples. Approximate Bayesian logistic regression models using penalized likelihood estimation via data augmentation were used to assess factors associated with HBsAg and HCVAb positivity; adjusted odds ratios (aOR) and 95% profile-likelihood limits (PLL) from these models are reported. The mean age was 25 years (IQR: 22–31; range:18–68); 14.4% ( n = 106) of participants were TGW. Prevalence of HBsAg was 3.4% (25/729; RDS adjusted: 2.7%,95%CI: 1.2% – 4.2%). Most, 92.0%(23/25) had detectable HBV DNA, the median HBV DNA was 3.0 (IQR:1.9–3.7) log 10 IU/ml. Age (aOR:1.04; 95%PLL:1.00-1.09), having sexual partners who ever injected drugs (aOR:3.13; 95%PLL:1.51–6.53), and reporting STI symptoms in the year preceding study entry (aOR:2.11; 95% PLL:1.03–4.34) were associated with HBsAg positivity. HCVAb was detected in 1.6% (12/729; RDS adjusted: 1.7% (95%CI: 0.7% − 2.7%), including 33.3% (4/12) with detectable HCV RNA. Age was the only factor significantly associated with HCVAb positivity (aOR:1.06; 95%PLL:1.01–1.12). Ongoing screening for viral hepatitis among MSM and TGW in Rwanda is needed and can strengthen viral hepatitis prevention, diagnosis, treatment, and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) screening programs. The prevalence of HBV and HCV was highest among MSM/TGW aged 35 and older(HBsAg 7.6%; HCVAb 3.8%), a group in which we previously found higher prevalence of HIV and other STIs. Interventions tailored to the needs of older MSM/TGW are needed. Finally, the high burden of HBV among individuals whose partners inject drugs suggests that the networks of MSM/TGW who inject drugs overlap with the networks of those who do not inject drugs. For optimal public health impact, MSM/TGW programming should consider addressing both injection drug use and sex-related acquisition and transmission risks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterize the epidemiology of hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) among men who have sex with men (MSM) and transgender women (TGW) in Kigali, Rwanda, to inform prevention and treatment programs.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted in Kigali, Rwanda, using respondent-driven sampling (RDS) between March and August 2018, involving 729 MSM/TGW participants. The mean age was 25 years (IQR: 22–31; range:18–68), with 14.4% (n = 106) being TGW.
Core methods
Stored plasma samples were tested using rapid diagnostic tests for HBV surface antigen (HBsAg) and HCV antibodies (HCVAb). Viral load testing was performed for all positive samples. Approximate Bayesian logistic regression models with penalized likelihood estimation via data augmentation were used to assess factors associated with HBsAg and HCVAb positivity.
Main results
HBsAg prevalence was 3.4% (25/729; RDS adjusted: 2.7%, 95%CI: 1.2% – 4.2%). Of these, 92.0% (23/25) had detectable HBV DNA, with a median of 3.0 (IQR:1.9–3.7) log10 IU/ml. HCVAb was detected in 1.6% (12/729; RDS adjusted: 1.7%, 95%CI: 0.7% − 2.7%), including 33.3% (4/12) with detectable HCV RNA.
Interpretation and novelty
Age, having sexual partners who ever injected drugs, and reporting STI symptoms in the year preceding study entry were associated with HBsAg positivity. Age was the only factor significantly associated with HCVAb positivity, highlighting the need for targeted interventions in older MSM/TGW.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of ongoing screening for viral hepatitis among MSM and TGW in Rwanda, particularly for those aged 35 and older, to strengthen prevention, diagnosis, treatment, and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) screening programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study used stored samples and relied on self-reported data, which may introduce recall bias. The evidence is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and potential limitations in capturing dynamic risk behaviors.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

慢性乙型肝炎患者中性功能障碍、抑郁症状及HBV传染性对性生活影响的评估:一项横断面研究

Sexual dysfunction, depressive symptoms, and perceived impact of HBV infectivity among noncirrhotic patients with chronic hepatitis B: a cross-sectional study
作者： Cheng-Run Song, Jing Zhou, Jiang-Nan Peng, Yu-Jing Li, Sahar Muzammal, En‐Qiang Chen
摘要中文翻译背景 慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)仍是全球主要公共卫生负担,但性健康在常规治疗中很少被关注。本研究旨在评估CHB患者中性功能障碍(SD)的患病率,并探讨与性功能相关的流行病学、临床和心理因素。我们还研究了性功能与HBV传染性对性生活负面影响的感知之间的关联。方法 本项横断面研究于2023年12月至2024年7月进行,纳入了657名CHB患者和121名非HBV对照者。根据治疗状态,CHB患者被分为三组:接受聚乙二醇干扰素α-2b治疗(n=391)、接受核苷(酸)类似物治疗(n=203)或未接受抗病毒药物治疗(n=63)。男性性功能通过IIEF-5、EHS和PEDT评估,女性则通过FSFI评估。心理状态通过PHQ-9和GAD-7评估,HBV传染性对性生活的负面影响则通过一个探索性单选是/否问题进行评估。采用分层多元线性回归分析以确定与性功能评分相关的因素。结果 所有CHB亚组中性功能障碍的患病率均显著高于非HBV组,其中IFN-CHB组的患病率最高,男性为70.9%,女性为70.8%。在多变量模型中,年龄较大、IFN-CHB状态以及较高的PHQ-9评分均与两性较低的性功能评分相关。在所研究的变量中,IFN-CHB状态在男性中显示出最强的负相关(β=-0.267,p<0.001),而PHQ-9评分在女性中显示出最强的负相关(β=-0.312,p=0.006)。在CHB患者中,有21.6%的男性和24.7%的女性报告了HBV传染性对性生活产生负面影响的感知。报告该感知的男性IIEF-5评分较低(p<0.001),报告该感知的女性FSFI评分较低(p=0.020)。结论 在性活跃的CHB患者中,性功能障碍较为常见,较差的性功能与IFN-CHB状态、抑郁症状以及HBV传染性对性生活的负面影响感知相关。因此,在全面的CHB治疗中,评估性健康和提供心理健康支持可能具有重要价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者中性功能障碍(SD)的患病率及其与流行病学、临床和心理因素的关联,以满足对理解CHB对性健康影响的需求,这种影响在常规治疗中常被忽视。
设计与对象
本项横断面研究于2023年12月至2024年7月进行,纳入了657名CHB患者和121名非HBV对照者,根据治疗状态将CHB患者分为三组:接受聚乙二醇干扰素α-2b治疗、接受核苷(酸)类似物治疗或未接受抗病毒药物治疗。
核心方法
男性性功能通过IIEF-5、EHS和PEDT评估,女性则通过FSFI评估。心理状态通过PHQ-9和GAD-7评估,HBV传染性对性生活的负面影响则通过一个探索性单选是/否问题进行评估。采用分层多元线性回归分析以确定与性功能评分相关的因素。
主要结果
所有CHB亚组中性功能障碍的患病率均显著高于非HBV组,其中IFN-CHB组的患病率最高。年龄较大、IFN-CHB状态以及较高的PHQ-9评分均与两性较低的性功能评分相关。在CHB患者中,有21.6%的男性和24.7%的女性报告了HBV传染性对性生活产生负面影响的感知。
解释与创新
在CHB患者中,性功能障碍较为常见,较差的性功能与IFN-CHB状态、抑郁症状以及HBV传染性对性生活的负面影响感知相关。本研究强调了在全面的CHB治疗中评估性健康和心理健康的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在管理CHB时,评估性健康和提供心理健康支持可能具有价值,因为它们可以改善患者的生活质量,并可能影响治疗决策。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其横断面设计,无法确定因果关系,且依赖于自我报告的测量方法。CHB亚组的样本量和多样性也可能影响结果的普遍性。

Sexual dysfunction, depressive symptoms, and perceived impact of HBV infectivity among noncirrhotic patients with chronic hepatitis B: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Cheng-Run Song, Jing Zhou, Jiang-Nan Peng, Yu-Jing Li, Sahar Muzammal, En‐Qiang Chen
Original abstract (English)
Background Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a major global public health burden, yet sexual health is rarely addressed in routine care. This study aimed to assess the prevalence of sexual dysfunction (SD) and examine demographic, clinical, and psychological factors associated with sexual function among noncirrhotic patients with CHB. We also explored the association between sexual function and the perceived negative impact of HBV infectivity on sexual life. Methods This cross-sectional study was conducted from December 2023 to July 2024, enrolling 657 patients with CHB and 121 non-HBV controls. Patients with CHB were classified by treatment status as receiving pegylated interferon α-2b ( n = 391), receiving nucleos(t)ide analogues ( n = 203), or not receiving antiviral therapy ( n = 63). Sexual function was assessed using the IIEF-5, EHS, and PEDT in men and the FSFI in women. Psychological status was assessed with the PHQ-9 and GAD-7, and the perceived negative impact of HBV infectivity on sexual life was assessed using a single exploratory yes/no item. Hierarchical multiple linear regression was used to identify factors associated with sexual function scores. Results The prevalence of SD was significantly higher in all CHB subgroups than in Non-HBV group, with the highest prevalence observed in the IFN-CHB group, both in men (70.9%) and women (70.8%). In multivariable models, older age, IFN-CHB status, and higher PHQ-9 scores were associated with lower sexual function scores in both sexes. Of the variables examined, IFN-CHB status showed the strongest negative association in men ( β = −0.267, p < 0.001), whereas PHQ-9 score showed the strongest negative association in women ( β = −0.312, p = 0.006). A perceived negative impact of HBV infectivity on sexual life was reported by 21.6% of men and 24.7% of women with CHB. Men reporting this perception had lower IIEF-5 scores ( p < 0.001), and women reporting it had lower FSFI scores ( p = 0.020). Conclusion Sexual dysfunction was common among sexually active patients with CHB, and poorer sexual function was associated with IFN-CHB status, depressive symptoms, and the perceived negative impact of HBV infectivity on sexual life. Sexual health assessment and mental health support may therefore have value in comprehensive CHB care.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the prevalence of sexual dysfunction (SD) and its association with demographic, clinical, and psychological factors among noncirrhotic patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB), addressing the need to understand the impact of CHB on sexual health, which is often overlooked in routine care.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted from December 2023 to July 2024, enrolling 657 patients with CHB and 121 non-HBV controls, with patients classified by treatment status into three groups: receiving pegylated interferon α-2b, receiving nucleos(t)ide analogues, or not receiving antiviral therapy.
Core methods
Sexual function was assessed using the IIEF-5, EHS, and PEDT in men and the FSFI in women, while psychological status was evaluated with the PHQ-9 and GAD-7. The perceived negative impact of HBV infectivity on sexual life was assessed using a single exploratory yes/no item. Hierarchical multiple linear regression was used to identify factors associated with sexual function scores.
Main results
The prevalence of SD was significantly higher in CHB subgroups compared to the non-HBV group, with the highest prevalence observed in the IFN-CHB group. Older age, IFN-CHB status, and higher PHQ-9 scores were associated with lower sexual function scores in both sexes. A perceived negative impact of HBV infectivity on sexual life was reported by 21.6% of men and 24.7% of women with CHB.
Interpretation and novelty
Sexual dysfunction was common among CHB patients, and poorer sexual function was associated with IFN-CHB status, depressive symptoms, and the perceived negative impact of HBV infectivity on sexual life. This study highlights the importance of assessing sexual health and mental health in comprehensive CHB care.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that sexual health assessment and mental health support may have value in managing CHB, as they can improve patient quality of life and potentially influence treatment decisions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which does not establish causality, and the reliance on self-reported measures. The sample size and diversity of CHB subgroups may also affect generalizability.

免疫抑制治疗后乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)异常复激活致死亡:一例具有非典型HBV血清学特征的病例报告

Fatal Hepatitis B Virus Reactivation Following Immunosuppressive Therapy in a Patient with an Atypical Hepatitis B Virus Serological Profile: A Case Report
作者： Fei-Ta Liu, Xiao-Qing Fu, Hai-Yang Yu, Chun-Tao Liu, Shou-Rong Liu, Rui Wu
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)复激活是淋巴瘤患者接受免疫抑制治疗时常见的并发症,尤其是含有利妥昔单抗的治疗方案。我们描述了一例58岁男性患者,其基线HBV血清学特征为非典型:乙型肝炎表面抗体(anti-HBs)阳性(102.00 U/L),乙型肝炎核心抗体阴性,乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)低水平(0.35 COI,当地实验室报告为阴性),且HBV DNA不可检测。在第四周期改良R-CHOP(利妥昔单抗、环磷酰胺、吡柔比星、长春瑞滨和地塞米松)化疗免疫治疗开始后的13天,anti-HBs滴度降至1.31 S/CO,HBsAg水平降至0.02 S/CO,而HBV DNA直至复激活才被重新评估。患者在治疗结束后约16周时出现疲劳、食欲减退和黄疸症状。HBsAg从28.66 S/CO升至8048.30 IU/mL,乙型肝炎e抗原转为阳性,HBV DNA升至1.94 × 109 IU/mL。尽管接受了替诺福韦艾拉酚胺和血浆置换治疗,患者仍因难治性肝衰竭和肝性脑病死亡。这些发现表明,非典型的血清学特征可能无法可靠地预示在强烈免疫抑制治疗期间发生严重HBV复激活的低风险。在治疗期间和治疗后进行全面的预治疗风险评估,并长期监测anti-HBs滴度、HBsAg和HBV DNA,可能有助于更早地发现和干预。

Fatal Hepatitis B Virus Reactivation Following Immunosuppressive Therapy in a Patient with an Atypical Hepatitis B Virus Serological Profile: A Case Report

Authors: Fei-Ta Liu, Xiao-Qing Fu, Hai-Yang Yu, Chun-Tao Liu, Shou-Rong Liu, Rui Wu
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation is a well-recognized complication in patients with lymphoma receiving immunosuppressive therapy, particularly rituximab-containing regimens. We describe a 58-year-old man with diffuse large B-cell lymphoma and an atypical baseline HBV serological profile: hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) positivity (102.00 U/L), antibody to hepatitis B core antigen negativity, a low-level hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) result (0.35 COI, reported as negative by the local laboratory), and undetectable HBV DNA. Thirteen days after initiation of the fourth cycle of modified R-CHOP (rituximab, cyclophosphamide, pirarubicin, vinorelbine and dexamethasone) chemoimmunotherapy, the anti-HBs titer had decreased to 1.31 S/CO, the HBsAg level to 0.02 S/CO, and HBV DNA was not reassessed until reactivation. Approximately 16 weeks after treatment completion, the patient developed fatigue, anorexia, and jaundice. HBsAg increased from 28.66 S/CO to 8048.30 IU/mL, hepatitis B e antigen became positive, and HBV DNA increased to 1.94 × 109 IU/mL. Despite treatment with tenofovir alafenamide fumarate and plasma exchange, the patient died of refractory liver failure and hepatic encephalopathy. These findings suggest that atypical serological profiles may not reliably indicate a low risk of severe HBV reactivation during intensive immunosuppressive therapy. Comprehensive pretreatment risk assessment and long-term monitoring of anti-HBs titers, HBsAg, and HBV DNA during and after therapy may facilitate earlier detection and intervention.
View research seven-element analysis

半定量超声评分揭示慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)在接受替诺福韦艾拉酚胺治疗期间肝脏结构恢复和免疫调节

Semi-quantitative ultrasound scoring reveals hepatic structural recovery and immune modulation during tenofovir alafenamide therapy in chronic hepatitis B
作者： Wen-Juan Li, Li-Hui Yang, Juan He
摘要中文翻译本研究旨在利用半定量超声(US)评分系统评估替诺福韦艾拉酚胺二甲基磺酸盐(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)患者中的治疗反应,并探讨超声影像变化与免疫功能恢复之间的相关性。方法 入选了100例接受TAF单药治疗(25 mg/天)至少24个月的慢性乙型肝炎患者进行回顾性分析。在基线、12个月和24个月时计算了包含肝脏回声、包膜轮廓、边缘形态、血管清晰度、门静脉直径(PVD)、脾脏厚度(ST)和脾静脉血流速度(SVV)的七参数半定量超声评分。同时测量了血清乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)脱氧核糖核酸(DNA)、丙氨酸氨基转移酶(ALT)、天门冬氨酸氨基转移酶(AST)以及外周T细胞亚群,包括分化簇3阳性(CD3+)、CD4+、CD8+ T细胞、CD4+/CD8+比值和自然杀伤(NK)细胞活性。分析了超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的相关性。结果 在24个月时,所有患者均达到HBV DNA <20 IU/mL,ALT和AST的正常化率分别为92%和95%。综合超声评分从基线的7.86 ± 1.73显著下降至24个月的3.37 ± 0.95(P < 0.001),其中包膜平滑度和实质均匀性的改善最为显著。CD4+ T细胞比例和CD4+/CD8+比值增加,而CD8+ T细胞比例下降;NK细胞活性从42.7 ± 7.3%上升至52.4 ± 6.2%(P < 0.05)。超声评分与ALT(r = 0.56)和AST(r = 0.47)呈正相关,与CD4+/CD8+比值(r = −0.46)和NK细胞活性(r = −0.38)呈负相关(所有P < 0.01)。结论 这些发现表明,TAF不仅抑制病毒复制并使肝酶正常化,还促进可测量的肝脏结构改善,这体现在半定量超声评分的下降。超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的强相关性支持其作为非侵入性影像生物标志物用于监测治疗疗效和肝脏修复的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在利用半定量超声(US)评分系统评估替诺福韦艾拉酚胺二甲基磺酸盐(TAF)在慢性乙型肝炎(慢性乙型肝炎)患者中的治疗反应,并探讨超声影像变化与免疫功能恢复之间的相关性。
设计与对象
入选了100例接受TAF单药治疗(25 mg/天)至少24个月的慢性乙型肝炎患者进行回顾性分析。研究持续时间为24个月,在基线、12个月和24个月时计算超声评分。
核心方法
计算了包含肝脏回声、包膜轮廓、边缘形态、血管清晰度、门静脉直径(PVD)、脾脏厚度(ST)和脾静脉血流速度(SVV)的七参数半定量超声评分。同时测量了血清乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)脱氧核糖核酸(DNA)、丙氨酸氨基转移酶(ALT)、天门冬氨酸氨基转移酶(AST)以及外周T细胞亚群,包括分化簇3阳性(CD3+)、CD4+、CD8+ T细胞、CD4+/CD8+比值和自然杀伤(NK)细胞活性。
主要结果
在24个月时,所有患者均达到HBV DNA <20 IU/mL,ALT和AST的正常化率分别为92%和95%。综合超声评分从基线的7.86 ± 1.73显著下降至24个月的3.37 ± 0.95(P < 0.001),其中包膜平滑度和实质均匀性的改善最为显著。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,TAF不仅抑制病毒复制并使肝酶正常化,还促进可测量的肝脏结构改善,这体现在半定量超声评分的下降。
科研与公卫意义
超声评分与生化和免疫参数之间的强相关性支持其作为非侵入性影像生物标志物用于监测治疗疗效和肝脏修复的潜力。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其回顾性设计和可能存在的选择偏倚,但由于观察到了显著的统计结果和明确的相关性,其证据是强有力的。

Semi-quantitative ultrasound scoring reveals hepatic structural recovery and immune modulation during tenofovir alafenamide therapy in chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Wen-Juan Li, Li-Hui Yang, Juan He
Original abstract (English)
Objective This study aimed to evaluate the therapeutic response to tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) using a semi-quantitative ultrasound (US) scoring system, and to investigate the correlation between sonographic changes and immune function restoration. Methods A total of 100 CHB patients who received TAF monotherapy (25 mg/day) for at least 24 months were retrospectively enrolled. A seven-parameter semi-quantitative US score, encompassing liver echogenicity, capsular contour, margin shape, vessel clarity, portal vein diameter (PVD), spleen thickness (ST), and splenic vein velocity (SVV), was calculated at baseline, 12 months, and 24 months. Concurrently, serum hepatitis B virus (HBV) deoxyribonucleic acid (DNA), alanine aminotransferase (ALT), aspartate aminotransferase (AST), peripheral T-cell subsets, including cluster of differentiation 3-positive (CD3 + ), CD4 + , CD8 + T cells, CD4+/CD8+ ratio, and natural killer (NK) cell activity were measured. Correlations between US scores and biochemical/immune parameters were analyzed. Results At 24 months, all patients achieved HBV DNA <20 IU/mL, with ALT and AST normalization rates of 92% and 95%, respectively. The comprehensive US score decreased significantly from 7.86 ± 1.73 at baseline to 3.37 ± 0.95 at 24 months ( P < 0.001), with the most pronounced improvements observed in capsular smoothness and parenchymal homogeneity. The CD4 + T-cell proportion and CD4 + /CD8 + ratio increased, whereas the CD8 + proportion decreased; NK cell activity rose from 42.7 ± 7.3% to 52.4 ± 6.2% ( P < 0.05). The US score was positively correlated with ALT (r = 0.56) and AST (r = 0.47), and negatively correlated with the CD4 + /CD8 + ratio (r = −0.46) and NK cell activity (r = −0.38) (all P < 0.01). Conclusion These findings indicate that TAF not only suppresses viral replication and normalises liver enzymes but also promotes measurable structural liver improvement, as reflected by declining semi-quantitative US scores. The strong correlations of US scores with biochemical and immune parameters support its utility as a non-invasive imaging biomarker for monitoring treatment efficacy and hepatic repair.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the therapeutic response to tenofovir alafenamide fumarate (TAF) in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) using a semi-quantitative ultrasound (US) scoring system, and to investigate the correlation between sonographic changes and immune function restoration.
Design and population
A total of 100 CHB patients who received TAF monotherapy (25 mg/day) for at least 24 months were retrospectively enrolled. The study was conducted over a period of 24 months, with US scores calculated at baseline, 12 months, and 24 months.
Core methods
A seven-parameter semi-quantitative US score, encompassing liver echogenicity, capsular contour, margin shape, vessel clarity, portal vein diameter (PVD), spleen thickness (ST), and splenic vein velocity (SVV), was calculated. Concurrently, serum hepatitis B virus (HBV) deoxyribonucleic acid (DNA), alanine aminotransferase (ALT), aspartate aminotransferase (AST), and peripheral T-cell subsets, including cluster of differentiation 3-positive (CD3 + ), CD4 + , CD8 + T cells, CD4+/CD8+ ratio, and natural killer (NK) cell activity were measured.
Main results
At 24 months, all patients achieved HBV DNA <20 IU/mL, with ALT and AST normalization rates of 92% and 95%, respectively. The comprehensive US score decreased significantly from 7.86 ± 1.73 at baseline to 3.37 ± 0.95 at 24 months (P < 0.001), with the most pronounced improvements observed in capsular smoothness and parenchymal homogeneity.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings indicate that TAF not only suppresses viral replication and normalises liver enzymes but also promotes measurable structural liver improvement, as reflected by declining semi-quantitative US scores.
Scientific and public-health significance
The strong correlations of US scores with biochemical and immune parameters support its utility as a non-invasive imaging biomarker for monitoring treatment efficacy and hepatic repair.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias, but the evidence is strong due to the significant statistical results and clear correlations observed.

慢性乙型肝炎患者筛查依从性与肝细胞癌分期之间的因果分析:一项回顾性队列研究研究

Casual analysis between screening adherence and hepatocellular carcinoma staging in hepatitis B patients: a retrospective cohort study
作者： Yi-Fan Ke, Ming-Xue Yu, Hong Yang, Xin-Hua Li, Yutian Chong, Yu-Feng Zhang, 崇雨田
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
当前临床指南建议慢性乙型肝炎患者每六个月进行一次腹部超声和甲胎蛋白检测。然而,大多数患者筛查依从性不足。
设计与对象
这项单中心、观察性、回顾性队列研究研究纳入了2008年至2023年间在中山大学附属第三医院感染科接受治疗的150名与慢性乙型肝炎相关的肝细胞癌患者。
核心方法
构建多变量回归模型以评估因果关联。混杂因素包括年龄、性别、肝硬化、核苷(类)类似物治疗、饮酒、乙型肝炎e抗原、乙型肝炎病毒 DNA和甲胎蛋白。
主要结果
Weighted logistic regression revealed that screening adherence was an independent protective factor against advanced hepatocellular carcinoma (OR, 0.209; 95% CI, 0.061–0.716; p = 0.013). Weighted multiple linear regression demonstrated a significant inverse correlation between screening adherence and maximum tumor diameter (coefficient, −1.851; 95% CI, −3.137 to −0.565; p = 0.005).
解释与创新
筛查依从性被定义为定期半年筛查的完成率。它被计算为(6 × 检查次数)÷ 随访月数。
科研与公卫意义
慢性乙型肝炎患者肝细胞癌筛查依从性不足会导致错过最佳诊断窗口,显著增加晚期癌症的风险。
局限与证据强度
采用稳定逆概率加权方法来平衡混杂因素。在逆概率加权后,所有混杂因素的标准化均值差异均小于0.1。

Casual analysis between screening adherence and hepatocellular carcinoma staging in hepatitis B patients: a retrospective cohort study

Authors: Yi-Fan Ke, Ming-Xue Yu, Hong Yang, Xin-Hua Li, Yutian Chong, Yu-Feng Zhang, 崇雨田
Original abstract (English)
Current clinical guidelines recommend that chronic hepatitis B patients undergo abdominal ultrasound and alpha-fetoprotein testing every six months. However, most of these patients exhibit insufficient screening adherence. This study aimed to determine whether screening adherence directly affects the tumor stage in patients with chronic hepatitis B-related hepatocellular carcinoma. This single-center, observational, retrospective cohort study enrolled 150 patients with chronic hepatitis B-related hepatocellular carcinoma who were treated at the Department of Infectious Diseases, Third Affiliated Hospital of Sun Yat-sen University, from 2008 to 2023. Stabilized inverse probability weighting was used to balance confounding factors. Screening adherence was defined as the completion rate of regular semiannual screenings. It was calculated as (6 × number of screenings) ÷ follow-up duration in months. The primary outcome measures were advanced hepatocellular carcinoma (China Liver Cancer Staging IIb–IV) and maximum tumor diameter. Multivariate regression models were constructed to assess causal associations. Confounding factors included age, gender, cirrhosis, nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy, alcohol consumption, hepatitis B e antigen, hepatitis B virus DNA, and alpha-fetoprotein. After inverse probability weighting, the standardized mean differences for all confounding factors were less than 0.1. Weighted logistic regression revealed that screening adherence was an independent protective factor against advanced hepatocellular carcinoma (OR, 0.209; 95% CI, 0.061–0.716; p = 0.013). Weighted multiple linear regression demonstrated a significant inverse correlation between screening adherence and maximum tumor diameter (coefficient, −1.851; 95% CI, −3.137 to −0.565; p = 0.005). Insufficient adherence to hepatocellular carcinoma screening among chronic hepatitis B patients results in missed optimal diagnostic windows. This significantly increases the risk of advanced cancer. All interested parties should collaborate to develop feasible strategies for improving patient adherence.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Current clinical guidelines recommend that chronic hepatitis B patients undergo abdominal ultrasound and alpha-fetoprotein testing every six months. However, most of these patients exhibit insufficient screening adherence.
Design and population
This single-center, observational, retrospective cohort study enrolled 150 patients with chronic hepatitis B-related hepatocellular carcinoma who were treated at the Department of Infectious Diseases, Third Affiliated Hospital of Sun Yat-sen University, from 2008 to 2023.
Core methods
Multivariate regression models were constructed to assess causal associations. Confounding factors included age, gender, cirrhosis, nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy, alcohol consumption, hepatitis B e antigen, hepatitis B virus DNA, and alpha-fetoprotein.
Main results
Weighted logistic regression revealed that screening adherence was an independent protective factor against advanced hepatocellular carcinoma (OR, 0.209; 95% CI, 0.061–0.716; p = 0.013). Weighted multiple linear regression demonstrated a significant inverse correlation between screening adherence and maximum tumor diameter (coefficient, −1.851; 95% CI, −3.137 to −0.565; p = 0.005).
Interpretation and novelty
Screening adherence was defined as the completion rate of regular semiannual screenings. It was calculated as (6 × number of screenings) ÷ follow-up duration in months.
Scientific and public-health significance
Insufficient adherence to hepatocellular carcinoma screening among chronic hepatitis B patients results in missed optimal diagnostic windows. This significantly increases the risk of advanced cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
Stabilized inverse probability weighting was used to balance confounding factors. After inverse probability weighting, the standardized mean differences for all confounding factors were less than 0.1.

一种结合比较可解释人工智能的贝叶斯优化异构堆叠框架用于乙型肝炎诊断支持

A Bayesian-Optimized Heterogeneous Stacking Framework with Comparative Explainable AI for Hepatitis B Diagnostic Support
作者： Sandeep Kumar, Mohit Arora
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒(乙型肝炎病毒,HBV)的诊断需要综合解读病毒载量标志物、生化参数、人口统计信息和临床症状。当可用的临床数据集较小且模型开发涉及架构选择、超参数优化、阈值选择和可解释性分析时,开发一个可靠的机器学习模型具有挑战性。本研究开发了Bayes Stack-HBV,这是一种旨在为乙型肝炎分类提供内部验证决策支持的贝叶斯优化异构堆叠框架。该框架使用了九个预测因子,包含216份患者记录,并通过弹性网络正则化的逻辑元学习器将异构基础学习器进行组合。在每个外层折叠训练集内,使用40次Optuna试验优化模型架构和超参数。通过重复嵌套交叉验证(三个随机种子,五个外层折叠,三个内层折叠)评估模型性能。预处理、SMOTE、模型优化和决策阈值选择仅限于训练和内层验证数据。Bayes Stack-HBV与Default Stack-HBV、历史梯度提升、支持向量机、随机森林、逻辑回归和K-近邻算法进行了比较。使用保留预测进行校准、成对统计比较和多方法可解释性分析。在15次保留的外层折叠评估中,Bayes Stack-HBV达到了0.9337 ± 0.0324的准确率、0.9357 ± 0.0307的平衡准确率、0.9659 ± 0.0383的精确率、0.9114 ± 0.0670的灵敏度、0.9600 ± 0.0471的特异度、0.9357 ± 0.0331的F1分数、0.8728 ± 0.0602的MCC、0.9844 ± 0.0162的ROC-AUC、0.9892 ± 0.0103的PR-AUC以及0.0428 ± 0.0188的Brier分数。Bayes Stack-HBV在十个平均评估指标中的七个指标上排名第一,表明其具有较强的特异性和校准导向的性能特征。然而,在多重比较校正后,与最强的集成比较模型之间的差异并不具有统计学意义。可解释性分析确定了HBVDNA和ALT水平作为有影响力的预测因子,这一结论得到了扩展的解释稳定性分析和特征依赖性分析的支持。Bayes Stack-HBV作为一种阈值感知且可解释的框架,为HBV决策支持提供了有前景的初步证据。其临床实用性和阈值迁移性需要通过更大规模、独立、多中心和前瞻性验证研究来确认。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒(乙型肝炎病毒,HBV)的诊断需要综合解读病毒载量标志物、生化参数、人口统计信息和临床症状。
设计与对象
本研究开发了Bayes Stack-HBV,这是一种旨在为乙型肝炎分类提供内部验证决策支持的贝叶斯优化异构堆叠框架。该框架使用了九个预测因子,包含216份患者记录,并通过弹性网络正则化的逻辑元学习器将异构基础学习器进行组合。
核心方法
在每个外层折叠训练集内,使用40次Optuna试验优化模型架构和超参数。预处理、SMOTE、模型优化和决策阈值选择仅限于训练和内层验证数据。Bayes Stack-HBV与Default Stack-HBV、历史梯度提升、支持向量机、随机森林、逻辑回归和K-近邻算法进行了比较。
主要结果
在15次保留的外层折叠评估中,Bayes Stack-HBV达到了0.9337 ± 0.0324的准确率、0.9357 ± 0.0307的平衡准确率、0.9659 ± 0.0383的精确率、0.9114 ± 0.0670的灵敏度、0.9600 ± 0.0471的特异度、0.9357 ± 0.0331的F1分数、0.8728 ± 0.0602的MCC、0.9844 ± 0.0162的ROC-AUC、0.9892 ± 0.0103的PR-AUC以及0.0428 ± 0.0188的Brier分数。
解释与创新
Bayes Stack-HBV在十个平均评估指标中的七个指标上排名第一,表明其具有较强的特异性和校准导向的性能特征。
科研与公卫意义
Bayes Stack-HBV作为一种阈值感知且可解释的框架,为HBV决策支持提供了有前景的初步证据。
局限与证据强度
其临床实用性和阈值迁移性需要通过更大规模、独立、多中心和前瞻性验证研究来确认。

A Bayesian-Optimized Heterogeneous Stacking Framework with Comparative Explainable AI for Hepatitis B Diagnostic Support

Authors: Sandeep Kumar, Mohit Arora
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B diagnosis requires the combined interpretation of viral-load markers, biochemical parameters, demographic information, and clinical symptoms. Developing a reliable machine-learning model for this purpose is challenging when the available clinical dataset is small and model development involves architecture selection, hyperparameter optimization, threshold selection, and explainability analysis. This study developed Bayes Stack-HBV, a Bayesian-optimized heterogeneous stacking framework intended to provide internally validated decision support for hepatitis B classification. The framework used nine predictors comprising two hundred sixteen patient records and combined heterogeneous base learners through an Elastic Net-regularized logistic meta-learner. Model architecture and hyperparameters were optimized using 40 Optuna trials within each outer-fold training set. Performance was evaluated using repeated nested cross-validation with three random seeds, five outer folds, and three inner folds. Preprocessing, SMOTE, model optimization, and decision-threshold selection were restricted to the training and inner-validation data. Bayes Stack-HBV was compared with Default Stack-HBV, Hist Gradient Boosting, Support Vector Machine, Extra Trees, Logistic Regression, and K-Nearest Neighbors. Calibration, paired statistical comparisons, and multi-method explainability analyses were performed using held-out predictions. Across 15 held-out outer-fold evaluations, Bayes Stack-HBV achieved an accuracy of 0.9337 ± 0.0324, balanced accuracy of 0.9357 ± 0.0307, precision of 0.9659 ± 0.0383, sensitivity of 0.9114 ± 0.0670, specificity of 0.9600 ± 0.0471, F1-score of 0.9357 ± 0.0331, MCC of 0.8728 ± 0.0602, ROC-AUC of 0.9844 ± 0.0162, PR-AUC of 0.9892 ± 0.0103, and Brier score of 0.0428 ± 0.0188. Bayes Stack-HBV ranked first on seven of ten mean evaluation measures, indicating a strong specificity- and calibration-oriented performance profile. However, differences relative to the strongest ensemble comparators were not statistically significant after multiple-comparison correction. Explainability analysis identified HBVDNA and ALT Level as influential predictors, supported by expanded explanation-stability and feature-dependency analyses. Bayes Stack-HBV provides promising preliminary evidence as a threshold-aware and interpretable framework for HBV decision support. Its clinical utility and threshold transportability require confirmation through larger, independent, multicenter, and prospective validation studies.
View research seven-element analysis

Hepatitis B diagnosis requires the combined interpretation of viral-load markers, biochemical parameters, demographic information, and clinical symptoms.
Design and population
This study developed Bayes Stack-HBV, a Bayesian-optimized heterogeneous stacking framework intended to provide internally validated decision support for hepatitis B classification. The framework used nine predictors comprising two hundred sixteen patient records and combined heterogeneous base learners through an Elastic Net-regularized logistic meta-learner.
Core methods
Model architecture and hyperparameters were optimized using 40 Optuna trials within each outer-fold training set. Preprocessing, SMOTE, model optimization, and decision-threshold selection were restricted to the training and inner-validation data. Bayes Stack-HBV was compared with Default Stack-HBV, Hist Gradient Boosting, Support Vector Machine, Extra Trees, Logistic Regression, and K-Nearest Neighbors.
Main results
Across 15 held-out outer-fold evaluations, Bayes Stack-HBV achieved an accuracy of 0.9337 ± 0.0324, balanced accuracy of 0.9357 ± 0.0307, precision of 0.9659 ± 0.0383, sensitivity of 0.9114 ± 0.0670, specificity of 0.9600 ± 0.0471, F1-score of 0.9357 ± 0.0331, MCC of 0.8728 ± 0.0602, ROC-AUC of 0.9844 ± 0.0162, PR-AUC of 0.9892 ± 0.0103, and Brier score of 0.0428 ± 0.0188.
Interpretation and novelty
Bayes Stack-HBV ranked first on seven of ten mean evaluation measures, indicating a strong specificity- and calibration-oriented performance profile.
Scientific and public-health significance
Bayes Stack-HBV provides promising preliminary evidence as a threshold-aware and interpretable framework for HBV decision support.
Limitations and evidence strength
Its clinical utility and threshold transportability require confirmation through larger, independent, multicenter, and prospective validation studies.

评论文章:癌症 监测 用于早期检测 HBV 感染患者的肝癌

Review Article: Cancer Surveillance for Early Detection of Liver Cancer in Patients with HBV Infection
作者： Andrew J. Park, Bai L. Pan, Xing-Fei Pan, James S. Park, Jia-Hong Dong, Calvin Q. Pan
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景和目的 慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的主要原因。尽管 抗病毒药物 治疗可减少肝硬化和肝衰竭,但 HBV 患者(包括非肝硬化肝脏)仍可能发展为 HCC。本综述旨在评估当前 HCC 监测 治疗 HBV 感染的策略,重点关注风险分层和早期检测的进展。方法 全面回顾 PubMed 和 Cochrane 数据库中发表的英文文献。这篇叙述性综述评估了 HCC 监测 策略在 HBV 感染患者中的有效性,重点关注早期 HCC 检测的最新进展。结果 本综述总结了与 HCC 易感性相关的病毒、宿主 和环境因素,并使用有或没有甲胎蛋白 (AFP) 的超声评估了当前的 监测。监测 资格应遵循指南定义的风险标准:估计年度 HCC 风险低于 0.2% 的患者通常不需要常规 监测,而应定期进行风险重新评估,而符合 监测 标准的患者应每 6 个月接受超声检查(伴或不伴 AFP)。风险预测分数可以完善评估,但应与早期检测测试区分开来。GALAD、HES 2.0、多目标血液检测和简化磁共振成像是有前途的早期检测方法,但许多支持证据来自混合病因学或非 HBV 队列研究,前瞻性 HBV 特异性验证仍然有限。结论 风险适应方法应将未来 HCC 风险的估计与 监测 和早期检测测试分开。符合指南资格的患者应每 6 个月接受一次 监测,而新兴的生物标志物、影像学和人工智能方法仍处于辅助或研究阶段,有待进一步的 HBV 特异性验证。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
慢性乙型肝炎 病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的主要原因。本综述旨在评估当前 HCC 监测 治疗 HBV 感染的策略,重点关注风险分层和早期检测的进展。
证据基础与方法
方法对PubMed和Cochrane数据库中发表的英文文献进行综合回顾。这篇叙述性综述评估了 HCC 监测 策略在 HBV 感染患者中的有效性,重点关注早期 HCC 检测的最新进展。
共识与结论
本综述总结了与 HCC 易感性相关的病毒、宿主 和环境因素,并使用有或没有甲胎蛋白 (AFP) 的超声评估当前的 监测。风险适应方法应将未来 HCC 风险的估计与 监测 和早期检测测试分开。
争议与缺口
风险预测分数可以完善评估,但应与早期检测测试区分开来。GALAD、HES 2.0、多目标血液检测和简化磁共振成像是有前途的早期检测方法,但许多支持证据来自混合病因学或非 HBV 队列研究,并且前瞻性 HBV 特异性验证仍然有限。
科研与实践启示
监测 资格应遵循指南定义的风险标准:估计年度 HCC 风险低于 0.2% 的患者通常不需要常规 监测,而应定期进行风险重新评估,而符合 监测 标准的患者应每 6 个月接受一次超声检查(伴或不伴 AFP)。符合指南资格的患者应每 6 个月接受一次 监测,而新兴的生物标志物、成像和人工智能方法仍处于辅助或研究阶段,等待进一步的 HBV 特异性验证。

Review Article: Cancer Surveillance for Early Detection of Liver Cancer in Patients with HBV Infection

Authors: Andrew J. Park, Bai L. Pan, Xing-Fei Pan, James S. Park, Jia-Hong Dong, Calvin Q. Pan
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background and Aims Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major cause of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). Although antiviral therapy reduces cirrhosis and liver failure, HCC may still develop in patients with HBV, including in non‐cirrhotic livers. This review aimed to evaluate current HCC surveillance strategies in HBV infection, focusing on risk stratification and advances in early detection. Methods English‐language literature published in PubMed and the Cochrane Database was comprehensively reviewed. This narrative review assessed the effectiveness of HCC surveillance strategies in HBV‐infected patients, with emphasis on recent advances in early‐stage HCC detection. Results This review summarizes viral, host and environmental factors associated with HCC susceptibility and evaluates current surveillance using ultrasound with or without alpha‐fetoprotein (AFP). Surveillance eligibility should follow guideline‐defined risk criteria: patients with an estimated annual HCC risk below 0.2% generally do not require routine surveillance and should undergo periodic risk reassessment, whereas patients meeting surveillance criteria should undergo ultrasound with or without AFP every 6 months. Risk‐prediction scores can refine assessment but should be distinguished from early‐detection tests. GALAD, HES 2.0, multi‐target blood tests, and abbreviated magnetic resonance imaging are promising early‐detection approaches, but much of the supporting evidence derives from mixed‐aetiology or non‐HBV cohorts and prospective HBV‐specific validation remains limited. Conclusion A risk‐adapted approach should separate estimation of future HCC risk from surveillance and early‐detection testing. Guideline‐eligible patients should undergo surveillance every 6 months, while emerging biomarker, imaging and artificial‐intelligence approaches remain adjunctive or investigational pending further HBV‐specific validation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major cause of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). This review aimed to evaluate current HCC surveillance strategies in HBV infection, focusing on risk stratification and advances in early detection.
Evidence base and method
Methods English-language literature published in PubMed and the Cochrane Database was comprehensively reviewed. This narrative review assessed the effectiveness of HCC surveillance strategies in HBV-infected patients, with emphasis on recent advances in early-stage HCC detection.
Consensus and conclusions
This review summarizes viral, host and environmental factors associated with HCC susceptibility and evaluates current surveillance using ultrasound with or without alpha-fetoprotein (AFP). A risk-adapted approach should separate estimation of future HCC risk from surveillance and early-detection testing.
Controversies and gaps
Risk-prediction scores can refine assessment but should be distinguished from early-detection tests. GALAD, HES 2.0, multi-target blood tests, and abbreviated magnetic resonance imaging are promising early-detection approaches, but much of the supporting evidence derives from mixed-aetiology or non-HBV cohorts and prospective HBV-specific validation remains limited.
Research and practice implications
Surveillance eligibility should follow guideline-defined risk criteria: patients with an estimated annual HCC risk below 0.2% generally do not require routine surveillance and should undergo periodic risk reassessment, whereas patients meeting surveillance criteria should undergo ultrasound with or without AFP every 6 months. Guideline-eligible patients should undergo surveillance every 6 months, while emerging biomarker, imaging and artificial-intelligence approaches remain adjunctive or investigational pending further HBV-specific validation.

高 治疗 依从性与 慢性乙型肝炎 和肝硬化患者观察到的较低 HCC 风险相关:一项全国性基于索赔的 队列研究 研究

High Treatment Adherence Is Associated With Lower Observed HCC Risk in Patients With Chronic Hepatitis B and Cirrhosis: A Nationwide Claims‐Based Cohort Study
作者： Yoon E. Shin, Jae Young Kim, Jeong Ju Yoo, Sang Gyune Kim, Young Seok Kim
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
摘要 坚持 抗病毒药物 治疗对于 慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 的病毒抑制至关重要,但其对肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的作用仍不确定。
证据基础与方法
使用药物持有率来衡量依从性,高依从性定义为≥90%,主要结局是HCC 发病率。
共识与结论
总共纳入 28 448 例患者,其中 19 001 例(66.8%)依从性较高,9447 例(33.2%)依从性较低。
争议与缺口
尽管这种关联在多项分析中仍然很明显,但不能完全排除残余混杂、治疗 选择效应、时间相关偏差和竞争风险问题。
科研与实践启示
由大韩民国健康保险审查和评估服务机构确定,患有 治疗 的初治 CHB 成人开始 TAF 或 ETV。

High Treatment Adherence Is Associated With Lower Observed HCC Risk in Patients With Chronic Hepatitis B and Cirrhosis: A Nationwide Claims‐Based Cohort Study

Authors: Yoon E. Shin, Jae Young Kim, Jeong Ju Yoo, Sang Gyune Kim, Young Seok Kim
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Adherence to antiviral therapy is essential for viral suppression in chronic hepatitis B (CHB), yet its effect on hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) remains uncertain. We examined the association between nucleos(t)ide analog (NA) adherence and HCC risk, focusing on tenofovir alafenamide (TAF) versus entecavir (ETV). Treatment‐naïve adults with CHB initiating TAF or ETV were identified from the Health Insurance Review and Assessment Service, Republic of Korea. Adherence was measured using the medication possession ratio, with high adherence defined as ≥ 90%, and the primary outcome was HCC incidence. A total of 28 448 patients were included, of whom 19 001 (66.8%) showed high adherence and 9447 (33.2%) showed low adherence. In the overall cohort, HCC incidence was comparable between the high‐ and low‐adherence groups (14.89 vs. 13.76 per 1000 person‐years; IRR, 1.08; 95% CI, 0.95–1.24). Among patients with claims‐defined cirrhosis, however, high adherence was associated with lower observed HCC incidence (32.92 vs. 49.59 per 1000 person‐years; IRR, 0.66; 95% CI, 0.57–0.78), and this association remained significant in multivariable Cox regression (HR, 0.77; 95% CI, 0.66–0.90) as well as in IPTW, landmark, and sensitivity analyses. In exploratory regimen‐stratified analyses, high‐adherence TAF users showed a lower observed cumulative HCC incidence than high‐adherence ETV users, whereas no significant difference was observed in the low‐adherence group. Logistic regression identified ETV use as a predictor of low adherence (OR, 2.80; 95% CI, 2.65–2.96). High adherence to antiviral therapy was associated with lower observed HCC incidence among patients with CHB and claims‐defined cirrhosis. Although this association remained evident across multiple analyses, residual confounding, treatment‐selection effects, time‐related bias, and competing‐risk issues cannot be fully excluded.
View review five-element analysis

Consensus and conclusions
A total of 28 448 patients were included, of whom 19 001 (66.8%) showed high adherence and 9447 (33.2%) showed low adherence.
Controversies and gaps
Although this association remained evident across multiple analyses, residual confounding, treatment‐selection effects, time‐related bias, and competing‐risk issues cannot be fully excluded.
Research and practice implications

致癌病毒的神经并发症:机制、临床表现和新见解。

Neurological Complications of Oncogenic Viruses: Mechanisms, Clinical Manifestations, and Emerging Insights.
作者： Lei Liu
摘要中文翻译致癌病毒因其在恶性转化中的关键作用而被广泛认可。然而,它们引起神经系统疾病的能力相对较少受到关注。越来越多的证据表明,多种致癌病毒,包括人 T 细胞白血病病毒 1 型 (HTLV-1)、人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)、乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV)、丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 和卡波西肉瘤相关疱疹病毒 (KSHV),与涉及中枢和周围神经系统的广泛神经系统并发症有关。这些表现是通过多种机制产生的,包括直接病毒神经侵袭、持续感染、免疫介导的组织损伤、慢性神经炎症和神经血管完整性破坏。此外,病毒诱导的分子改变,如氧化应激、线粒体功能障碍、表观遗传重塑和异常的细胞因子信号传导,会导致神经元功能障碍和疾病进展。在这篇综述中,我们通过整合分子、实验和临床研究的证据,批判性地研究了目前对与主要致癌病毒相关的神经并发症的理解。我们首先讨论病毒感染引起神经损伤的一般机制,强调嗜神经病毒和非嗜神经病毒之间共享的途径。然后,我们总结了与每种致癌病毒相关的神经学表现和病毒特异性致病机制,强调神经发病机制中常见和独特的分子途径。最后,我们解决当前的诊断挑战、新兴生物标志物和治疗策略,同时确定限制因果解释和临床转化的关键知识差距。全面了解致癌病毒、免疫系统和神经系统之间复杂的相互作用将有助于早期 诊断,改善患者管理,并支持开发针对病毒相关神经系统疾病的靶向治疗方法。

Neurological Complications of Oncogenic Viruses: Mechanisms, Clinical Manifestations, and Emerging Insights.

Authors: Lei Liu
Original abstract (English)
Oncogenic viruses are widely recognized for their pivotal role in malignant transformation; however, their capacity to induce neurological disorders has received comparatively less attention. Increasing evidence indicates that several oncogenic viruses, including human T-cell leukemia virus type 1 (HTLV-1), human papillomavirus (HPV), hepatitis B virus (HBV), hepatitis C virus (HCV), and Kaposi's sarcoma-associated herpesvirus (KSHV), are associated with a broad spectrum of neurological complications involving both the central and peripheral nervous systems. These manifestations arise through diverse mechanisms, including direct viral neuroinvasion, persistent infection, immune-mediated tissue injury, chronic neuroinflammation, and disruption of neurovascular integrity. Moreover, virus-induced molecular alterations, such as oxidative stress, mitochondrial dysfunction, epigenetic remodelling, and aberrant cytokine signalling, contribute t neuronal dysfunction and disease progression. In this review, we critically examine the current understanding of the neurological complications associated with major oncogenic viruses by integrating evidence from molecular, experimental, and clinical studies. We first discuss the general mechanisms by which viral infections induce neurological injury, highlighting pathways shared among neurotropic and non-neurotropic viruses. We then summarise the neurological manifestations and virus-specific pathogenic mechanisms associated with each oncogenic virus, emphasising both common and distinct molecular pathways underlying neuropathogenesis. Finally, we address current diagnostic challenges, emerging biomarkers, and therapeutic strategies while identifying critical knowledge gaps that limit causal interpretation and clinical translation. A comprehensive understanding of the complex interactions between oncogenic viruses, the immune system, and the nervous system will facilitate earlier diagnosis, improve patient management, and support the development of targeted therapeutic approaches for virus-associated neurological disorders.

Oncogenic viruses are widely recognized for their pivotal role in malignant transformation; however, their capacity to induce neurological disorders has received comparatively less attention.
Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions
These manifestations arise through diverse mechanisms, including direct viral neuroinvasion, persistent infection, immune-mediated tissue injury, chronic neuroinflammation, and disruption of neurovascular integrity.
Controversies and gaps
Moreover, virus-induced molecular alterations, such as oxidative stress, mitochondrial dysfunction, epigenetic remodelling, and aberrant cytokine signalling, contribute to neuronal dysfunction and disease progression.
Research and practice implications
A comprehensive understanding of the complex interactions between oncogenic viruses, the immune system, and the nervous system will facilitate earlier diagnosis, improve patient management, and support the development of targeted therapeutic approaches for virus-associated neurological disorders.

HBV/HCV合并感染与抗结核药物性肝损伤:从风险评估、机制探索到预防思考

HBV/HCV coinfection and anti-tuberculosis drug-induced liver injury: from risk assessment and exploration of mechanisms to preventive considerations
作者： Xiao-Jing Li, Na Zhang, Qiang Zhang, Rui Chen, Guo Yu
摘要中文翻译目的:抗结核药物引起的肝损伤(AT-DILI)是结核病 治疗 期间常见且具有临床意义的不良事件。先前的研究经常报告,同时感染 乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和/或丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 的患者发生 AT-DILI 的风险增加。本综述旨在探讨这种易感性升高的潜在机制,并讨论结核病和 HBV/HCV 合并感染患者的预防注意事项。方法:我们在 PubMed、Web of Science 和 Google Scholar 上进行了全面的文献检索,并辅以临床指南和权威报告,以确定截至 2026 年 8 月发表的相关研究。筛选并按主题组织相关文章,以综合有关风险估计、潜在机制和预防考虑因素的证据。结果:现有证据表明 HBV/HCV 合并感染与 AT-DILI 风险增加相关,其程度因病毒活动、基线肝病和病毒学指标选择而异。较高的病毒载量似乎与 AT-DILI 风险更密切相关。潜在的机制包括免疫炎症变化、药物代谢改变、肝储备减少和病毒特异性肝损伤,包括 HBV 再激活。这些观察结果为针对这些风险和潜在机制的预防策略提供了信息,包括基线肝脏和病毒学评估、动态监测、抗病毒药物治疗、个体化抗结核方案优化以及选择性使用保肝药物。结论:HBV/HCV 合并感染会增加 AT-DILI 的风险,值得临床积极关注。未来的努力应侧重于基于病毒学和肝脏状况的个体化预防,同时验证新兴生物标志物和基于人工智能的预测工具以完善风险识别。

HBV/HCV coinfection and anti-tuberculosis drug-induced liver injury: from risk assessment and exploration of mechanisms to preventive considerations

Authors: Xiao-Jing Li, Na Zhang, Qiang Zhang, Rui Chen, Guo Yu
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Anti-tuberculosis drug-induced liver injury (AT-DILI) is a common and clinically significant adverse event during tuberculosis treatment. Previous studies have frequently reported an increased risk of AT-DILI among patients coinfected with hepatitis B virus (HBV) and/or hepatitis C virus (HCV). This review aims to explore potential mechanisms underlying this heightened susceptibility and to discuss prevention considerations for patients with tuberculosis and HBV/HCV coinfection. METHODS: We conducted a comprehensive literature search across PubMed, Web of Science, and Google Scholar, supplemented by clinical guidelines and authoritative reports, to identify relevant studies published through August 2026. Relevant articles were screened and thematically organized to synthesize evidence on risk estimation, underlying mechanisms, and prevention considerations. RESULTS: Available evidence indicates that HBV/HCV coinfection is associated with increased AT-DILI risk, with the magnitude varying by viral activity, baseline liver disease, and virological indicator selection. Higher viral loads appear more closely associated with AT-DILI risk. Potential mechanisms include immune-inflammatory changes, altered drug metabolism, reduced hepatic reserve, and virus-specific liver injury, including HBV reactivation. These observations inform preventive strategies targeting these risks and potential mechanisms, including baseline hepatic and virological assessment, dynamic monitoring, antiviral therapy, individualized anti-tuberculosis regimen optimization, and selective use of hepatoprotective agents. CONCLUSION: HBV/HCV coinfection increases the risk of AT-DILI and warrants active clinical attention. Future efforts should focus on individualizing prevention based on virological and hepatic status, while validating emerging biomarkers and artificial intelligence-based predictive tools to refine risk identification.

Evidence base and method
We conducted a comprehensive literature search across PubMed, Web of Science, and Google Scholar, supplemented by clinical guidelines and authoritative reports, to identify relevant studies published through August 2026.
Consensus and conclusions
Higher viral loads appear more closely associated with AT-DILI risk.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
Future efforts should focus on individualizing prevention based on virological and hepatic status, while validating emerging biomarkers and artificial intelligence-based predictive tools to refine risk identification.

资源匮乏地区乙型和丙型肝炎的护理点诊断:叙述性回顾

Point of Care Diagnostics for Hepatitis B and C in Low-resource Settings: A Narrative Review
作者： Abhay Saraf, Sunita Girish, Rohini Adepwar
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 和丙型肝炎病毒 (HCV) 仍然是主要的公共卫生挑战,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC),实验室能力较差,早期发现疾病并有助于其管理的能力较差。正确、及时的诊断对于临床干预和全球消除至关重要。本叙述性综述探讨了使用即时护理 (POC) 检测来加强资源匮乏环境中 HBV 和 HCV 检测的潜力。审查了同行评审的文献、全球健康报告和灰色文献,以评估 POC 技术的最新技术、诊断性能、资源有限环境中的实施以及新的发展。还指出了改善及时有效的 HBV 和 HCV 诊断 的可及性的现有挑战和未来机遇。移动医疗集成、人工智能 (AI) 支持的解释和自我测试等新技术和功能增强了他们的能力。仍然存在一些挑战,包括政策采用不平衡、供应链问题和社会耻辱,导致难以扩展。POC 诊断有可能通过改善快速检测的机会来加速消除肝炎。它们的成功实施需要加强卫生系统、训练有素的医疗保健人员以及融入现有的医疗保健服务。

Point of Care Diagnostics for Hepatitis B and C in Low-resource Settings: A Narrative Review

Authors: Abhay Saraf, Sunita Girish, Rohini Adepwar
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B Virus (HBV) and Hepatitis C Virus (HCV) continue to be major public health challenge especially in Low- and MiddleIncome Countries (LMICs), with poor laboratory capacity and the ability to detect the disease earlier and contribute to its management. Proper and timely diagnosis is crucial for clinical intervention and global elimination. The present narrative review explores the potential for the use of Point-Of-Care (POC) testing to enhance the testing of HBV and HCV in low-resource settings. Peer-reviewed literature, global health reports, and grey literature were reviewed to assess the state of the art of POC technologies, diagnostic performance, and implementation in resource-limited settings, as well as new developments. Existing challenges and future opportunities for improving access to timely and effective HBV and HCV diagnosis are also pointed out. New technologies and features, such as mobile healthcare integration, Artificial Intelligence (AI)-powered interpretation, and self-testing, enhance their capabilities. There are still areas of challenge, including uneven policy adoption, supply chain issues, and social stigma, that make it difficult to scale. POC diagnostics have the potential to accelerate hepatitis elimination by improving access to rapid testing. Their successful implementation requires strengthened health systems, trained healthcare workers, and integration into existing healthcare services.
View review five-element analysis

Hepatitis B Virus (HBV) and Hepatitis C Virus (HCV) continue to be major public health challenge especially in Low- and MiddleIncome Countries (LMICs), with poor laboratory capacity and the ability to detect the disease earlier and contribute to its management.
Evidence base and method
Peer-reviewed literature, global health reports, and grey literature were reviewed to assess the state of the art of POC technologies, diagnostic performance, and implementation in resource-limited settings, as well as new developments.
Consensus and conclusions
POC diagnostics have the potential to accelerate hepatitis elimination by improving access to rapid testing. Their successful implementation requires strengthened health systems, trained healthcare workers, and integration into existing healthcare services.
Controversies and gaps
There are still areas of challenge, including uneven policy adoption, supply chain issues, and social stigma, that make it difficult to scale.
Research and practice implications
Existing challenges and future opportunities for improving access to timely and effective HBV and HCV diagnosis are also pointed out.

肯尼亚四个县医疗工作者完成乙型肝炎疫苗接种接种计划的情况:一项混合方法研究

Completion of Hepatitis B Vaccination Schedule Among Healthcare Workers in Four Counties in Kenya: A Mixed-Methods Study
作者： Harriet K. Mirieri, Jeanette Dawa, Dalmas Ochieng Omia, Rose Jalang’o, Rosalia Kalani, Cynthia Ombok, Sheila Libendi, Wendy Tirop, Elizabeth Kiptoo, Stephen Wandei
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:医疗工作者(HCWs)因职业暴露而面临更高的乙型肝炎病毒感染风险,但在肯尼亚完成推荐的乙型肝炎疫苗接种接种计划仍不理想。我们评估了医疗工作者完成三剂疫苗接种接种计划的情况、相关因素、动机和障碍。方法:我们开展了一项混合方法研究,结合了在肯尼亚卡卡梅加、纳库鲁、蒙巴萨和西亚县178家医疗机构中对1614名医疗工作者的横断面调查,以及九次焦点小组讨论。调查于2023年8月至9月进行,焦点小组讨论于2024年6月至2025年1月进行。采用带有医疗机构随机截距的混合效应逻辑回归模型,以确定完成三剂疫苗接种接种计划的相关因素,并对定性数据进行了主题分析。结果:在1583名已知疫苗接种状态的医疗工作者中,700人(44.2%;95% CI:41.8–46.7)报告已完成三剂接种,410人(25.9%)接种了一剂或两剂,473人(29.9%)从未接种过疫苗。主要动机是自我保护(71.7%)。常见的障碍包括疫苗不可用(57.5%)、缺乏知识(28.8%)、疫苗未在机构内提供(28.2%)以及费用(17.4%)。完成接种的比例在男性医疗工作者、在城市地区工作的医疗工作者以及有6–10年或11–20年医疗工作经验的医疗工作者中更高,而在支持人员、行政人员、临床医生和相关医疗专业人员中较低,相较于护士/助产士。结论:在研究的四个县中,不到一半的医疗工作者报告完成了三剂乙型肝炎疫苗接种接种计划。提高疫苗的可获得性、降低自费费用、加强接种提醒系统以及解决知识差距可能有助于提高这些县医疗工作者的接种完成率。

范围与问题
乙型肝炎病毒感染、基因型和疾病。与生物相关动物、分类学、模型、差异诊断和比较实体有关的内容为补充性,除非有明确针对目标的证据;只有显式的词汇或无关的硬性排除才是最终的。
证据基础与方法
该综述类型是一项结合横断面调查和焦点小组讨论的混合方法研究。研究在肯尼亚的四个县进行,时间从2023年8月至9月,以及2024年6月至2025年1月。纳入标准包括在178家医疗机构工作的医疗工作者(HCWs)。纳入研究的数量为一项,方法包括带有医疗机构随机截距的混合效应逻辑回归分析以及对定性数据的主题分析。
共识与结论
在1583名已知疫苗接种状态的医疗工作者中,44.2%报告已完成乙型肝炎疫苗接种接种计划的三剂。主要动机是自我保护,主要障碍是疫苗不可用、缺乏知识和费用。

完成乙型肝炎疫苗接种接种计划的比例在男性医疗工作者、在城市地区工作的医疗工作者以及有6–10年或11–20年医疗工作经验的医疗工作者中更高。支持人员和行政人员的完成率低于护士/助产士。该研究强调了提高疫苗可获得性、降低自费费用以及解决知识差距的必要性。
科研与实践启示
提高疫苗的可获得性、降低自费费用、加强接种提醒系统以及解决知识差距可能有助于提高研究县医疗工作者的接种完成率。

Completion of Hepatitis B Vaccination Schedule Among Healthcare Workers in Four Counties in Kenya: A Mixed-Methods Study

Authors: Harriet K. Mirieri, Jeanette Dawa, Dalmas Ochieng Omia, Rose Jalang’o, Rosalia Kalani, Cynthia Ombok, Sheila Libendi, Wendy Tirop, Elizabeth Kiptoo, Stephen Wandei
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Healthcare workers (HCWs) are at an increased risk of hepatitis B virus infection through occupational exposure, yet completion of the recommended hepatitis B vaccination schedule remains suboptimal in Kenya. We assessed vaccination completion, associated factors, motivators, and barriers among HCWs. Methods: We conducted a mixed-methods study combining a cross-sectional survey of 1614 HCWs in 178 health facilities across Kakamega, Nakuru, Mombasa, and Siaya counties with nine focus group discussions. The survey was conducted in August–September 2023 and the focus groups during June 2024–January 2025. Mixed-effects logistic regression with a random intercept for health facility was used to identify factors associated with completion of three vaccine doses, and qualitative data were analyzed thematically. Results: Among 1583 HCWs with a known vaccination status, 700 (44.2%; 95% CI: 41.8–46.7) reported completing three doses, 410 (25.9%) had received one or two doses, and 473 (29.9%) had never been vaccinated. The main motivator was self-protection (71.7%). Common barriers were vaccine unavailability (57.5%), lack of knowledge (28.8%), the vaccine not being offered on site (28.2%), and cost (17.4%). Completion was more likely among male HCWs, those working in urban areas facilities, and those with 6–10 or 11–20 years of healthcare work experience, and less likely among support staff, administrative staff, clinicians and allied health professionals than among nurses/midwives. Conclusions: Fewer than half of HCWs reported completing the three-dose hepatitis B vaccination schedule. Improving vaccine availability, reducing out-of-pocket costs, strengthening dose-reminder systems, and addressing knowledge gaps may improve completion among HCWs in the study counties.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Hepatitis B virus infection, genotypes and disease. Biologically related animal, taxonomic, model, differential-diagnosis and comparative entities are supplementary-only unless material target-specific evidence is also present; only explicit lexical or unrelated hard exclusions are terminal.
Evidence base and method
The review type is a mixed-methods study combining a cross-sectional survey and focus group discussions. The study was conducted in four counties in Kenya from August–September 2023 and June 2024–January 2025. The eligibility criteria included healthcare workers (HCWs) in 178 health facilities. The included-study count is one, and the methods included mixed-effects logistic regression and thematic analysis of qualitative data.
Consensus and conclusions
Among 1583 HCWs with a known vaccination status, 44.2% reported completing three doses of the hepatitis B vaccination schedule. The main motivator was self-protection, and the main barriers were vaccine unavailability, lack of knowledge, and cost.
Controversies and gaps
Completion of the hepatitis B vaccination schedule was more likely among male HCWs, those working in urban areas, and those with 6–10 or 11–20 years of healthcare work experience. Support staff and administrative staff had lower completion rates compared to nurses/midwives. The study highlights the need for improving vaccine availability, reducing out-of-pocket costs, and addressing knowledge gaps.
Research and practice implications
Improving vaccine availability, reducing out-of-pocket costs, strengthening dose-reminder systems, and addressing knowledge gaps may improve completion among HCWs in the study counties.

Global prevalence of hepatitis B among displaced populations (internally-displaced persons, refugees, and asylum seekers): a systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Efosa Ekhator, Oluwatosin Rachael Afolabi, Moses Ikpomwonsa Okundaye, Victor Godwin Essien, Micheal Ayodeji Amodu, Joel Idemudia Omolumen
摘要中文翻译

范围与问题
本系统综述和荟萃分析评估了全球乙型肝炎在流离失所者及其亚组中的患病率。

共识与结论

Global prevalence of hepatitis B among displaced populations (internally-displaced persons, refugees, and asylum seekers): a systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Efosa Ekhator, Oluwatosin Rachael Afolabi, Moses Ikpomwonsa Okundaye, Victor Godwin Essien, Micheal Ayodeji Amodu, Joel Idemudia Omolumen
Original abstract (English)
Displaced persons, including internally displaced persons (IDPs), refugees and asylum seekers, are vulnerable to several diseases, including hepatitis B. This systematic review and meta-analysis assessed the global prevalence of hepatitis B among displaced persons and their subgroups. The Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) 2020 guideline was followed in this review. Relevant published studies were searched from eight academic databases from 2016 to 2026, with additional citation searching. After screening, relevant data were extracted from each included study and assessed using the Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) checklist. Using Stata 15.0, the random-effects model of DerSimonian and Laird, with the Freeman-Tukey double arcsine transformation, was used to pool the estimates. Heterogeneity was assessed using I 2 and Cochran’s Q test, and the sources were traced using subgroup and meta-regression analyses. The leave-out-one sensitivity analysis was used to test study’s robustness. Publication bias was assessed using Egger regression test and funnel plot visualisation. The search strategy identified 4,673 records, and 117 were included. The pooled prevalence of hepatitis B was 3.86% (95% confidence interval [CI]: 3.45–4.29%). The prevalence was higher among males, 5.98% (CI: 4.61–7.50%), compared to females, 3.82% (CI: 2.96–4.78%), while children and adolescents recorded lower rates at 1.56% (CI: 1.01–2.20%). The prevalence was highest among those from Eastern Asia, 11.62% (CI: 7.61–16.32%), South-eastern Asia, 7.18% (CI: 5.70–8.80%), and Sub-Saharan Africa, 6.62% (CI: 5.44–7.90%). Subgroup analysis indicated significantly higher ( p ≤ 0.05) pooled prevalence among IDPs, 9.99% (CI: 6.82–13.67%), those hosted in Africa, 7.74% (CI: 5.02–10.97%), and in displacement camps, 7.19% (CI: 5.47–9.11%). Considerable heterogeneity remained after subgroup analyses and multivariate meta-regression linked the heterogeneity to displacement status, male percentage, temporal design and study settings. Sensitivity analysis demonstrated the robustness of findings, while Egger’s regression test and funnel plot asymmetry indicated small-study effect. In conclusion, displaced persons have a high burden of hepatitis B compared to the general population, with being male, an IDP, of Eastern Asian origin, residing in displacement camps, and being from Africa associated with higher risk. Hence, government and non-governmental organisations are encouraged to prioritise them in their hepatitis B control efforts.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This systematic review and meta-analysis assessed the global prevalence of hepatitis B among displaced persons and their subgroups.
Evidence base and method
The Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) 2020 guideline was followed in this review.
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled prevalence of hepatitis B was 3.86% (95% confidence interval [CI]: 3.45–4.29%).
Controversies and gaps
Considerable heterogeneity remained after subgroup analyses and multivariate meta-regression linked the heterogeneity to displacement status, male percentage, temporal design and study settings.
Research and practice implications
Hence, government and non-governmental organisations are encouraged to prioritise them in their hepatitis B control efforts.

抗乙型肝炎病毒和丙型肝炎病毒的药用植物体外和体内研究:一项叙述性综述

In vitro and in vivo studies of medicinal plants against hepatitis B and hepatitis C infections: A narrative review
作者： Shabila Lintang Ramadhani, Tutik Sri Wahyuni, Achmad Fuad Hafid, Chie Aoki-Utsubo, Youdiil Ophinni
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)和丙型肝炎病毒(HCV)感染仍然是全球主要的健康问题。本综述总结了当前关于具有抗病毒药物活性的药用植物对HBV和HCV作用的证据,重点探讨了实验模型和潜在的分子机制。采用叙述性综述的方法,通过与肝炎病毒和药用植物相关的关键词,在PubMed、Scopus、ScienceDirect和Google Scholar中识别并呈现相关研究。相关研究的选择基于其与主题的相关性,特别是那些在体外和体内模型中报告抗病毒药物活性的研究。通过按植物种类、提取物类型、病毒靶点以及抗病毒药物作用机制来组织证据,对研究结果进行了叙述性综合。药用植物含有多种具有抗病毒药物潜力的生物活性化合物,包括黄酮类、生物碱、查尔酮、多糖和多酚。体外和体内研究的证据表明,这些化合物可能抑制病毒附着和进入,抑制聚合酶活性,减少乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)和乙型肝炎e抗原(HBeAg)的分泌,干扰衣壳组装,并抑制HCV非结构蛋白。一些显著的化合物,如表没食子儿茶素没食子酸酯(EGCG)、水飞蓟素、槲皮素衍生物和茄碱,已在实验模型中显示出降低病毒核酸水平并改善与肝脏相关结果的潜力。由于其多靶点生物活性化合物,药用植物在对抗HBV和HCV方面具有成为抗病毒药物来源的前景。然而,提取物的变异性以及临床证据有限,需要进一步标准化和临床研究以确认其治疗用途。

范围与问题
采用叙述性综述的方法,通过与肝炎病毒和药用植物相关的关键词,在PubMed、Scopus、ScienceDirect和Google Scholar中识别并呈现相关研究。
证据基础与方法
相关研究的选择基于其与主题的相关性,特别是那些在体外和体内模型中报告抗病毒药物活性的研究。
共识与结论
体外和体内研究的证据表明,这些化合物可能抑制病毒附着和进入,抑制聚合酶活性,减少乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)和乙型肝炎e抗原(HBeAg)的分泌,干扰衣壳组装,并抑制HCV非结构蛋白。
争议与缺口
然而,提取物的变异性以及临床证据有限,需要进一步标准化和临床研究以确认其治疗用途。
科研与实践启示
由于其多靶点生物活性化合物,药用植物在对抗HBV和HCV方面具有成为抗病毒药物来源的前景。

In vitro and in vivo studies of medicinal plants against hepatitis B and hepatitis C infections: A narrative review

Authors: Shabila Lintang Ramadhani, Tutik Sri Wahyuni, Achmad Fuad Hafid, Chie Aoki-Utsubo, Youdiil Ophinni
Original abstract (English)
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) and hepatitis C virus (HCV) infections remain major global health problems. This review summarizes current evidence on medicinal plants with reported antiviral activity against HBV and HCV, focusing on experimental models and underlying molecular mechanisms. A narrative review approach was used to identify and present relevant studies from PubMed, Scopus, ScienceDirect, and Google Scholar using keywords related to hepatitis viruses and medicinal plants. Relevant studies were selected based on their relevance to the topic, particularly those reporting antiviral activity in in vitro and in vivo models. The findings were synthesized narratively by organizing the evidence according to plant species, extract types, viral targets, and mechanisms of antiviral action. Medicinal plants contained diverse bioactive compounds with antiviral potential, including flavonoids, alkaloids, chalcones, polysaccharides, and polyphenols. Evidence from in vitro and in vivo studies indicates that these compounds may inhibit viral attachment and entry, suppress polymerase activity, reduce hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg) secretion, interfere with capsid assembly, and inhibit HCV non-structural proteins. Notable compounds such as epigallocatechin gallate (EGCG), silibinin, quercetin derivatives, and solanopubamine demonstrated reductions in viral nucleic acid levels and improvements in liver-related outcomes in experimental models. Medicinal plants are promising antiviral sources against HBV and HCV due to their multi-target bioactive compounds. However, variability in extracts and limited clinical evidence require further standardized and clinical studies to confirm their therapeutic use.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
A narrative review approach was used to identify and present relevant studies from PubMed, Scopus, ScienceDirect, and Google Scholar using keywords related to hepatitis viruses and medicinal plants.
Evidence base and method
Relevant studies were selected based on their relevance to the topic, particularly those reporting antiviral activity in in vitro and in vivo models.
Consensus and conclusions
Evidence from in vitro and in vivo studies indicates that these compounds may inhibit viral attachment and entry, suppress polymerase activity, reduce hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and hepatitis B e antigen (HBeAg) secretion, interfere with capsid assembly, and inhibit HCV non-structural proteins.
Controversies and gaps
However, variability in extracts and limited clinical evidence require further standardized and clinical studies to confirm their therapeutic use.
Research and practice implications
Medicinal plants are promising antiviral sources against HBV and HCV due to their multi-target bioactive compounds.

基线乙型肝炎表面抗原和肝硬化在确定干扰素 治疗 持续时间方面的生物学解释

Biological interpretation of baseline hepatitis B surface antigen and cirrhosis in determining interferon treatment duration
作者： Ling-Feng Zou, Shao-Ding Yu, Yan Luo, Cheng-Long Wang
摘要中文翻译最近将基线乙型肝炎表面抗原和肝硬化确定为延长干扰素治疗的独立预测因子,提供了有价值的临床框架,但这些关联背后的生物学机制需要进一步阐明。在本意见综述中,我们不仅将这些因素解释为统计协变量,而且将其解释为病毒-宿主 平衡的病理替代物。我们认为,高基线乙型肝炎表面抗原可能反映了肝内根深蒂固的病毒 储存宿主,并可能创造一个富含抗原的环境,损害有效的 抗病毒药物 免疫力。同时,肝硬化可以更好地理解为一系列结构重塑,其中血管变化和肝窦开窗可能限制免疫 监测 并影响局部治疗途径。因此,可能需要(至少部分)超过 48 周的 治疗 持续时间来克服这些深刻的免疫学和结构障碍。我们主张将未来的预测模型从二元临床定义转变为病毒动力学和组织学形态的精细整合,以完善 慢性乙型肝炎 中的精准医学。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
最近发现基线乙型肝炎表面抗原和肝硬化是延长干扰素治疗的独立预测因子,为临床提供了有价值的框架,但这些关联背后的生物学机制仍需进一步阐明。
证据基础与方法
在本观点综述中,我们认为这些因素不仅仅是统计学上的协变量,而是病毒与宿主平衡的病理替代标志。
共识与结论
我们提出,较高的基线乙型肝炎表面抗原水平可能反映了乙型肝炎病毒在肝内高度固着的储存宿主,并可能形成抗原丰富的环境,从而损害有效的抗病毒药物免疫应答。
争议与缺口
同时,肝硬化可能更好地被理解为一种结构重塑的谱系,在其中血管变化和窦状隙的破坏可能限制免疫监测,并影响局部治疗的可及性。
科研与实践启示
因此,可能需要(至少部分)超过 48 周的 治疗 持续时间来克服这些深刻的免疫学和结构障碍。

Biological interpretation of baseline hepatitis B surface antigen and cirrhosis in determining interferon treatment duration

Authors: Ling-Feng Zou, Shao-Ding Yu, Yan Luo, Cheng-Long Wang
Original abstract (English)
The recent identification of baseline hepatitis B surface antigen and cirrhosis as independent predictors of extended interferon therapy provides a valuable clinical framework, yet the biological mechanisms underlying these associations require further elucidation. In this opinion review, we interpret these factors not merely as statistical covariates but as pathological surrogates for the virus-host equilibrium. We propose that high baseline hepatitis B surface antigen may reflect a deeply entrenched intrahepatic viral reservoir and may create an antigen-rich environment that impairs effective antiviral immunity. Concurrently, cirrhosis may be better understood as a spectrum of architectural remodeling in which vascular changes and sinusoidal defenestration could limit immune surveillance and affect local therapeutic access. Consequently, treatment durations exceeding 48 weeks may be required, at least in part, to overcome these profound immunological and structural barriers. We advocate shifting future predictive modeling from binary clinical definitions to a granular integration of viral kinetics and histological morphology to refine precision medicine in chronic hepatitis B.

Hubungan Determinan Sosial Kesehatan dengan Ketepatan Waktu Skrining Hepatitis B pada Ibu Hamil

Hubungan Determinan Sosial Kesehatan dengan Ketepatan Waktu Skrining Hepatitis B pada Ibu Hamil

Published: 2026-10-01  |  Publisher: International Agency for Research on Cancer (IARC)
A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

IARC 2026肝癌意识月 - 国际癌症研究机构(IARC)

IARC marks Liver Cancer Awareness Month 2026 - International Agency for Research on Cancer (IARC)
新闻简报国际癌症研究机构(IARC)标示2026年肝癌意识月,强调肝癌的全球负担。根据最新估计,2024年有近850,000人患上了肝癌,记录了超过730,000例死亡。IARC报告指出,乙型肝炎病毒感染是主要的致病因素,估计有520,000例与之相关。该机构还指出,肝癌是全球第七常见的癌症类型,也是第三常见的癌症死亡原因。IARC科学家将于2026年10月14日举办网络研讨会,讨论肝癌预防的研究优先事项,重点关注预防中的证据缺口和紧迫问题。该活动是更广泛努力的一部分,旨在提高意识并促进有效的预防策略,包括针对乙型肝炎的疫苗接种计划。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该活动于2026年10月1日启动,网络研讨会定于2026年10月14日举行。
地点与对象
全球范围,特别提到亚洲地区的肝癌发病率最高,而大洋洲地区最低。
事件
IARC开展2026年肝癌意识月活动,强调肝癌的全球负担及其与乙型肝炎病毒发展的关系。
规模、影响与风险
2024年,几乎有850,000人患上了肝癌,超过730,000人死于肝癌。乙型肝炎病毒感染估计在2024年导致了约520,000例肝癌病例。
应对、状态与不确定性
IARC科学家将于2026年10月14日主持网络研讨会,讨论肝癌预防的研究优先事项。这些信息基于最新的全球癌症负担估计。
A high priorityPublic-health news

IARC marks Liver Cancer Awareness Month 2026 - International Agency for Research on Cancer (IARC)

News Brief
The International Agency for Research on Cancer (IARC) marks Liver Cancer Awareness Month 2026, highlighting the global burden of liver cancer. According to the most recent estimates, almost 850,000 people developed liver cancer in 2024, with over 730,000 deaths recorded. The IARC report emphasizes that infection with hepatitis B virus is a major contributor, with an estimated 520,000 cases linked to it. The agency also notes that liver cancer is the seventh most common cancer type worldwide and the third most common cause of cancer death. IARC scientists will host a webinar on 14 October 2026 to discuss research priorities for liver cancer prevention, focusing on evidence gaps and pressing issues in control. The event is part of broader efforts to raise awareness and promote effective prevention strategies, including vaccination programs against hepatitis B.
Original source text (English)

Click to change language EN FR iarc newsletter Donate now HOME Impact in Practice IARC@60 Cancer Topics Research and Programmes Media Centre Publications Training Events Careers About IARC More IARC IARC News Press Releases Featured News Videos and Podcasts Infographics and Photos Infographics Resource Centre Questions and Answers Events Contact 1 October 2026 Cancer statistics Infections Liver cancer Vaccination IARC marks Liver Cancer Awareness Month 2026 Almost 850 000 people developed liver cancer and more than 730 000 people died from the disease in 2024, according to the most recent estimates of the global cancer burden by researchers from the International Agency for Research on Cancer (IARC). Liver cancer is the seventh most common cancer type in the world and the third most common cause of cancer death. IARC scientists will lead a webinar at 15:00–16:00 CEST on 14 October titled “Research Priorities for Liver Cancer Prevention”, to mark Liver Cancer Awareness Month and present the latest research on the topic, the evidence gaps, and the most pressing issues related to liver cancer control. Liver cancer is more common among men, who are more than twice as likely as women to develop the disease. Large disparities exist across and within regions. Liver cancer is most common in Asia, where almost 600 000 new cases occurred in 2024. It is least common in Oceania, where fewer than 5000 new cases occurred that year. The risk of developing liver cancer is much higher in people with chronic infection with hepatitis A, B, C, D, or E. IARC recently classified hepatitis D virus as carcinogenic to humans (Group 1), alongside previous classifications of hepatitis B virus and hepatitis C virus in Group 1. Many cases of liver cancer can be prevented, and effective vaccination programmes exist to immunize against infection with hepatitis B virus. Effective therapy also exists to cure infection with hepatitis C virus. Infection with a cancer-causing hepatitis virus is estimated to have caused about 520 000 cases of liver cancer in 2024. View five news elements
Time
The event is marked on 1 October 2026, with a webinar scheduled for 14 October 2026.
Location and population
Global, with specific mention of Asia having the highest incidence of liver cancer and Oceania having the lowest.
Event
IARC marks Liver Cancer Awareness Month 2026, highlighting the global burden of liver cancer and the role of hepatitis viruses in its development.
Scale, impact and risk
In 2024, almost 850,000 people developed liver cancer, and more than 730,000 died from it. Hepatitis B virus infection is estimated to have caused about 520,000 cases of liver cancer in 2024.
Response, status and uncertainty
IARC scientists will lead a webinar on 14 October 2026 to discuss research priorities for liver cancer prevention. The information is based on the most recent global cancer burden estimates.
